	error("NOT RUBY V1 could not find PlayerGui after 10 seconds")
end

do
	for _,oldName in ipairs({"ClarkControlCenter","NotRubyV1"}) do
		local oldMenu = playerGui:FindFirstChild(oldName)
		if oldMenu then
			oldMenu:Destroy()
		end
	end
end
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
local character = player.Character
local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or nil
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or nil

-- Never hold the complete interface behind an infinite character wait. Features
-- can ask for a short wait when pressed, while the menu itself opens instantly.
local function loadCharacter(timeoutSeconds)
	timeoutSeconds = math.max(tonumber(timeoutSeconds) or 6,0)
	local deadline = os.clock() + timeoutSeconds

	repeat
		character = player.Character
		humanoid =
			character
			and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			or nil
		root =
			character
			and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			or nil

		if character and humanoid and root then
			return true
		end

		if os.clock() >= deadline then
			return false
		end

		task.wait(.05)
	until false
end
loadCharacter(0)

local function clampGuiObjectToViewport(object,margin)
	if not object or not object.Parent then
		return
	end

	margin = tonumber(margin) or 4
	camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport =
		camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1000,700)
	local size = object.AbsoluteSize
	local position = object.AbsolutePosition
	local maxX = math.max(margin,viewport.X-size.X-margin)
	local maxY = math.max(margin,viewport.Y-size.Y-margin)
	local clampedX = math.clamp(position.X,margin,maxX)
	local clampedY = math.clamp(position.Y,margin,maxY)
	if math.abs(clampedX-position.X) > .5
		or math.abs(clampedY-position.Y) > .5 then
		object.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(clampedX,clampedY)
	end
end

gui:SetAttribute("PanelScale",1)
local function setPanelScale(value,animate)
	value = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 1,.8,1.3)
	camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport =
		camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1000,700)
	local baseWidth = math.max(1,panel.Size.X.Offset)
	local baseHeight = math.max(1,panel.Size.Y.Offset)
	local maximumFitScale = math.max(
		.8,
		math.min(
			1.3,
			(viewport.X-8)/baseWidth,
			(viewport.Y-8)/baseHeight
		)
	)
	value = math.min(value,maximumFitScale)
	gui:SetAttribute("PanelScale",value)
	local scale = panel:FindFirstChild("SmoothScale")
	if not scale then
		return value
	end

	if animate ~= false
		and gui:GetAttribute("ReduceMotion") ~= true then
		TweenService:Create(
			scale,
			TweenInfo.new(.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{Scale = value}
		):Play()
	else
		scale.Scale = value
	end
	task.delay(animate == false and 0 or .2,function()
		if not menuDeleted
			and not panel:GetAttribute("MenuTransitionBusy") then
			clampGuiObjectToViewport(panel,4)
		end
	end)
	return value
end

local function corner(object,radius)
	local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
	c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,radius or 10)
	c.Parent = object
	return c
end
local function addStroke(object,color,thickness,transparency)
	local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	local outlineColor = gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent
	local selectedColor = color or outlineColor
	s.Color = selectedColor
	s.Thickness = thickness or 1
	s.Transparency = transparency or 0
	s:SetAttribute(
		"NotRubyFixedColor",
		selectedColor ~= accent and selectedColor ~= outlineColor
	)
	s.Parent = object
	return s
end

local function setStatus(text)
	if not status or not status.Parent then
		return
	end
	status.Text = text
	status.TextColor3 = accent
	status.TextTransparency = .36
	status.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(72,33)
	TweenService:Create(
		status,
		TweenInfo.new(.16,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{
			TextTransparency = 0,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(65,33)
		}
	):Play()

	local notifyEvent = gui:FindFirstChild("NotRubyNotifyEvent")
	if notifyEvent and notifyEvent:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		notifyEvent:Fire(tostring(text))
	end
end

local startupIssues = {}
local function safeSetup(name,callback)
	local ok,result = pcall(callback)
	if ok then
		return true,result
	end

	local message = tostring(name)..": "..tostring(result)
	table.insert(startupIssues,message)
	gui:SetAttribute("StartupIssueCount",#startupIssues)
	warn("[NOT RUBY] "..message)
	task.defer(function()
		if gui and gui.Parent and not menuDeleted then
			setStatus(string.upper(tostring(name)).." // SETUP FAILED")
		end
	end)
	return false,result
end

local function makePage(name)
	local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	page.Name = name.."Page"
	page.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	page.BorderSizePixel = 0
	page.ScrollBarThickness = 4
	page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent
	page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
	page.Visible = false
	page.Parent = content
	local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0,10)
	pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0,12)
	pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,10)
	pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,10)
	pad.Parent = page
	local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	layout.Padding = UDim.new(0,8)
	layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
	layout.Parent = page
	pages[name] = page
	return page
end
local function makeTab(name,text)
	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
	button.Name = name.."Tab"
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
	button.Text = ""
	button.TextSize = 1
	button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	button.TextColor3 = TEXT
	button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	button.BorderSizePixel = 0
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.Parent = sidebar
	corner(button,12)
	addStroke(button,accent,.7,.9)

	local icon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	icon.Name = "TabIcon"
	icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8,0)
	icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24,44)
	icon.Text = tabIcons[name] or "•"
	icon.TextSize = 14
	icon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	icon.TextColor3 = accent
	icon.Parent = button

	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.Name = "TabLabel"
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34,0)
	label.Size = UDim2.new(1,-40,1,0)
	label.Text = text
	label.TextSize = 9
	label.TextWrapped = true
	label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	label.TextColor3 = TEXT
	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	label.Parent = button

	local selectedIndicator = Instance.new("Frame")
	selectedIndicator.Name = "SelectedIndicator"
	selectedIndicator.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,.5)
	selectedIndicator.Position = UDim2.new(0,4,.5,0)
	selectedIndicator.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3,0)
	selectedIndicator.BackgroundColor3 = accent
	selectedIndicator.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selectedIndicator.BorderSizePixel = 0
	selectedIndicator.ZIndex = button.ZIndex + 1
	selectedIndicator.Parent = button
	corner(selectedIndicator,1000)

	local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	scale.Scale = 1
	scale.Parent = button

	button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.08,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = .96}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	tabButtons[name] = button
	return button
end
local function applyTabVisuals()
	do
		local line = header:FindFirstChild("AnimatedAccentLine")
		local glow = header:FindFirstChild("AnimatedAccentGlow")
		if line then
			line.BackgroundColor3 = accent
		end
		if glow then
			glow.BackgroundColor3 = accent
		end
	end

	for name,button in pairs(tabButtons) do
		local selected = name == currentTab
		local indicator = button:FindFirstChild("SelectedIndicator")
		local icon = button:FindFirstChild("TabIcon")
		local label = button:FindFirstChild("TabLabel")
		if indicator then
			indicator.BackgroundColor3 = accent
			TweenService:Create(
				indicator,
				TweenInfo.new(.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{
					Size = selected and UDim2.fromOffset(3,24) or UDim2.fromOffset(3,0),
					BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1
				}
			):Play()
		end
		TweenService:Create(
			button,
			TweenInfo.new(.14,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{
				BackgroundColor3 = selected and accent or SURFACE,
				TextColor3 = selected and ACTIVE_TEXT or TEXT
			}
		):Play()
		if icon and icon:IsA("TextLabel") then
			icon.TextColor3 = selected and ACTIVE_TEXT or accent
		end
		if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then
			label.TextColor3 = selected and ACTIVE_TEXT or TEXT
		end
	end

	for name,page in pairs(pages) do
		page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent

		if name == currentTab then
			page.Visible = true
			page.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10,0)
			page.ScrollBarImageTransparency = .55

			TweenService:Create(
				page,
				TweenInfo.new(.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{
					Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0),
					ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0
				}
			):Play()
		else
			page.Visible = false
		end
	end
end
local function setTab(name)
	if not pages[name] then
		return
	end
	currentTab = name
	applyTabVisuals()
	local displayName = name
	if name == "LOCUST" then
		displayName = "HOUSE OF THE LOCUST"
	elseif name == "HOME" then
		displayName = "UNIVERSAL"
	elseif name == "CREATOR" then
		displayName = "CREATOR"
	elseif name == "DASHBOARD" then
		displayName = "DASHBOARD"
	elseif name == "EXPLOITER" then
		displayName = "EXPLOITER DETECTOR"
	elseif name == "TROLL" then
		displayName = "TROLL"
	elseif name == "NAV" then
		displayName = "NAVIGATOR"
	elseif name == "ACCESS" then
		displayName = "ACCESSIBILITY"
	elseif name == "SYSTEM" then
		displayName = "SYSTEM LAB"
	end
	setStatus(displayName)
end

local function makeSection(page,titleText,description)
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,description and 72 or 50)
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	frame.Parent = page
	corner(frame,14)
	addStroke(frame,accent,.9,.76)

	local rail = Instance.new("Frame")
	rail.Name = "SectionAccent"
	rail.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,10)
	rail.Size = UDim2.new(0,3,1,-20)
	rail.BackgroundColor3 = accent
	rail.BorderSizePixel = 0
	rail.Parent = frame
	corner(rail,100)

	local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,SURFACE_2),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,SURFACE)
	})
	gradient.Rotation = 8
	gradient.Parent = frame
	local sectionTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	sectionTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	sectionTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,9)
	sectionTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,20)
	sectionTitle.Text = titleText
	sectionTitle.TextSize = 11
	sectionTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	sectionTitle.TextColor3 = TEXT
	sectionTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	sectionTitle.Parent = frame
	if description then
		local desc = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		desc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		desc.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,31)
		desc.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,32)
		desc.Text = description
		desc.TextWrapped = true
		desc.TextSize = 9
		desc.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		desc.TextColor3 = MUTED
		desc.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		desc.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		desc.Parent = frame
	end
	return frame
end
local function makeAction(page,text)
	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,46)
	button.Text = text
	button.TextSize = 10
	button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	button.TextColor3 = TEXT
	button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	button.BorderSizePixel = 0
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.Parent = page
	corner(button,12)
	addStroke(button,accent,.9,.78)
	local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,SURFACE_2),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,SURFACE)
	})
	gradient.Rotation = 12
	gradient.Parent = button

	local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	scale.Parent = button

	button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.07,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = .975}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	return button
end
local function makePair(page,leftText,rightText)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
	row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	row.Parent = page
	local left = Instance.new("TextButton")
	left.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-4,1,0)
	left.Text = leftText
	left.TextSize = 10
	left.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	left.TextColor3 = TEXT
	left.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	left.BorderSizePixel = 0
	left.AutoButtonColor = false
	left.Parent = row
	corner(left,11)
	addStroke(left,accent,.8,.84)
	local right = Instance.new("TextButton")
	right.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-4,1,0)
	right.Position = UDim2.new(.5,4,0,0)
	right.Text = rightText
	right.TextSize = 10
	right.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	right.TextColor3 = TEXT
	right.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	right.BorderSizePixel = 0
	right.AutoButtonColor = false
	right.Parent = row
	corner(right,11)
	addStroke(right,accent,.8,.84)

	for _,button in ipairs({left,right}) do
		local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
		scale.Parent = button

		button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(.07,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
					{Scale = .97}
				):Play()
			end
		end)

		button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
					{Scale = 1}
				):Play()
			end
		end)
	end

	return left,right
end
local function makeNumberRow(page,labelText,defaultText,buttonText)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,48)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.Parent = page
	corner(row,12)
	addStroke(row,accent,.7,.88)
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,0)
	label.Size = UDim2.new(.30,-6,1,0)
	label.Text = labelText
	label.TextSize = 9
	label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	label.TextColor3 = TEXT
	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	label.Parent = row
	local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
	box.Size = UDim2.new(.25,-5,0,34)
	box.Position = UDim2.new(.30,0,.5,-17)
	box.Text = defaultText
	box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	box.TextSize = 11
	box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	box.TextColor3 = TEXT
	box.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	box.BorderSizePixel = 0
	box.Parent = row
	corner(box,9)
	local apply = Instance.new("TextButton")
	apply.Size = UDim2.new(.45,-12,0,34)
	apply.Position = UDim2.new(.55,5,.5,-17)
	apply.Text = buttonText or "APPLY"
	apply.TextSize = 9
	apply.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	apply.TextColor3 = TEXT
	apply.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	apply.BorderSizePixel = 0
	apply.AutoButtonColor = false
	apply.Parent = row
	corner(apply,9)
	addStroke(apply,accent,.8,.86)

	local applyScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	applyScale.Parent = apply
	local boxStroke = addStroke(box,accent,1.2,1)

	apply.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				applyScale,
				TweenInfo.new(.07,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = .96}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	apply.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				applyScale,
				TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	box.Focused:Connect(function()
		boxStroke.Color = accent
		TweenService:Create(
			boxStroke,
			TweenInfo.new(.12),
			{Transparency = .1}
		):Play()
	end)

	box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		TweenService:Create(
			boxStroke,
			TweenInfo.new(.16),
			{Transparency = 1}
		):Play()
	end)

	return box,apply
end

local function showPlayerPicker(titleText,onSelected,includeSelf)
	local oldPicker = gui:FindFirstChild("NotRubyPlayerPicker")
	if oldPicker then
		oldPicker:Destroy()
	end

	local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
	overlay.Name = "NotRubyPlayerPicker"
	overlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
	overlay.BackgroundTransparency = .28
	overlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
	overlay.Active = true
	overlay.ZIndex = 950
	overlay.Parent = gui

	local card = Instance.new("Frame")
	card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
	card.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
	card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(410,390)
	card.BackgroundColor3 = BG
	card.BorderSizePixel = 0
	card.ZIndex = 951
	card.Parent = overlay
	corner(card,18)
	addStroke(card,accent,1.8,.02)

	local cardScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	cardScale.Scale = .9
	cardScale.Parent = card
	TweenService:Create(
		cardScale,
		TweenInfo.new(.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{Scale = 1}
	):Play()

	local pickerTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	pickerTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	pickerTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16,12)
	pickerTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-64,0,30)
	pickerTitle.Text = titleText or "SELECT PLAYER"
	pickerTitle.TextSize = 14
	pickerTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	pickerTitle.TextColor3 = TEXT
	pickerTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	pickerTitle.ZIndex = 952
	pickerTitle.Parent = card

	local closePicker = Instance.new("TextButton")
	closePicker.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34,34)
	closePicker.Position = UDim2.new(1,-46,0,10)
	closePicker.Text = "X"
	closePicker.TextSize = 13
	closePicker.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	closePicker.TextColor3 = TEXT
	closePicker.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(92,32,43)
	closePicker.AutoButtonColor = false
	closePicker.ZIndex = 952
	closePicker.Parent = card
	corner(closePicker,9)

	local search = Instance.new("TextBox")
	search.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,52)
	search.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,38)
	search.PlaceholderText = "Search username or display name"
	search.Text = ""
	search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	search.TextSize = 10
	search.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	search.TextColor3 = TEXT
	search.PlaceholderColor3 = MUTED
	search.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	search.BorderSizePixel = 0
	search.ZIndex = 952
	search.Parent = card
	corner(search,10)

	local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	list.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,100)
	list.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,1,-114)
	list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	list.BorderSizePixel = 0
	list.ScrollBarThickness = 3
	list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent
	list.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
	list.ZIndex = 952
	list.Parent = card

	local listLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,7)
	listLayout.Parent = list

	local rows = {}

	local function close()
		if overlay and overlay.Parent then
			overlay:Destroy()
		end
	end

	local function addPlayerRow(otherPlayer)
		if otherPlayer == player and not includeSelf then
			return
		end

		local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
		row.Name = "Player_"..tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
		row.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,62)
		row.Text = ""
		row.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
		row.BorderSizePixel = 0
		row.AutoButtonColor = false
		row.ZIndex = 953
		row.Parent = list
		corner(row,11)

		local rowScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
		rowScale.Parent = row

		local avatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		avatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46,46)
		avatar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8,8)
		avatar.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		avatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
		avatar.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
		avatar.ZIndex = 954
		avatar.Parent = row
		corner(avatar,23)
		addStroke(avatar,accent,1.2,.12)

		local displayName = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		displayName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		displayName.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(64,9)
		displayName.Size = UDim2.new(1,-76,0,20)
		displayName.Text = otherPlayer.DisplayName
		displayName.TextSize = 11
		displayName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		displayName.TextColor3 = TEXT
		displayName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		displayName.ZIndex = 954
		displayName.Parent = row

		local username = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		username.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		username.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(64,30)
		username.Size = UDim2.new(1,-76,0,18)
		username.Text = "@"..otherPlayer.Name.."  •  "..tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
		username.TextSize = 9
		username.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		username.TextColor3 = MUTED
		username.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		username.ZIndex = 954
		username.Parent = row

		rows[row] = {
			searchText = string.lower(otherPlayer.Name.." "..otherPlayer.DisplayName)
		}

		row.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				TweenService:Create(
					rowScale,
					TweenInfo.new(.07),
					{Scale = .97}
				):Play()
			end
		end)

		row.InputEnded:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(
				rowScale,
				TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end)

		row.Activated:Connect(function()
			close()
			if onSelected then
				onSelected(otherPlayer)
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok,image = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
					otherPlayer.UserId,
					Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
					Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
				)
			end)
			if ok and image and avatar.Parent then
				avatar.Image = image
			end
		end)
	end

	for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		addPlayerRow(otherPlayer)
	end

	search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local query = string.lower(search.Text)
		for row,data in pairs(rows) do
			if row and row.Parent then
				row.Visible =
					query == ""
					or string.find(data.searchText,query,1,true) ~= nil
			end
		end
	end)

	closePicker.Activated:Connect(close)
end

local function setupCreatorTab()
	local creatorTab = makeTab("CREATOR","CREATOR")
	local creatorPage = makePage("CREATOR")
	local creatorUsername = "werdx33xxxr5"
	local creatorDisplayName = "not ruby"
	local creatorUserId = 5717888103

	creatorTab.Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("CREATOR")
	end)

	makeSection(
		creatorPage,
		"CREATOR",
		"The creator profile is available directly in this clean build."
	)

	local card = Instance.new("Frame")
	card.Name = "CreatorProfileCard"
	card.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,238)
	card.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	card.BorderSizePixel = 0
	card.Parent = creatorPage
	corner(card,16)
	addStroke(card,accent,1.2,.16)

	local cardGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	cardGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,SURFACE_2),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(.62,SURFACE),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,BG)
	})
	cardGradient.Rotation = 20
	cardGradient.Parent = card

	local creatorPhoto = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	creatorPhoto.Name = "CreatorPhoto"
	creatorPhoto.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,18)
	creatorPhoto.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(104,104)
	creatorPhoto.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	creatorPhoto.BorderSizePixel = 0
	creatorPhoto.Image = ""
	creatorPhoto.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
	creatorPhoto.Parent = card
	corner(creatorPhoto,24)
	addStroke(creatorPhoto,accent,1.5,.04)

	local fallbackDragon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	fallbackDragon.Name = "FallbackDragon"
	fallbackDragon.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	fallbackDragon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	fallbackDragon.Text = "🐉"
	fallbackDragon.TextSize = 46
	fallbackDragon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	fallbackDragon.TextColor3 = TEXT
	fallbackDragon.Parent = creatorPhoto

	local display = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	display.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	display.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(140,22)
	display.Size = UDim2.new(1,-158,0,32)
	display.Text = creatorDisplayName
	display.TextSize = 20
	display.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	display.TextColor3 = TEXT
	display.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	display.Parent = card

	username.BackgroundTransparency = 1

	username.TextSize = 11

	username.Parent = card

	local userIdLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	userIdLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	userIdLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(140,82)
	userIdLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-158,0,20)
	userIdLabel.Text = "USER ID: "..tostring(creatorUserId)
	userIdLabel.TextSize = 9
	userIdLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	userIdLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
	userIdLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	userIdLabel.Parent = card

	local buildBadge = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	buildBadge.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(140,108)
	buildBadge.Size = UDim2.new(1,-158,0,28)
	buildBadge.BackgroundColor3 = accent
	buildBadge.BackgroundTransparency = .08
	buildBadge.BorderSizePixel = 0
	buildBadge.Text = "DRAGON REMAKE // CLEAN BUILD"
	buildBadge.TextSize = 8
	buildBadge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	buildBadge.TextColor3 = ACTIVE_TEXT
	buildBadge.Parent = card
	corner(buildBadge,9)

	local copyIdButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	copyIdButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,154)
	copyIdButton.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-22,0,48)
	copyIdButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	copyIdButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	copyIdButton.Text = "COPY CREATOR ID"
	copyIdButton.TextSize = 9
	copyIdButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	copyIdButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
	copyIdButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	copyIdButton.Parent = card
	corner(copyIdButton,11)
	addStroke(copyIdButton,accent,.9,.72)

	local refreshPhotoButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	refreshPhotoButton.Position = UDim2.new(.5,4,0,154)
	refreshPhotoButton.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-22,0,48)
	refreshPhotoButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	refreshPhotoButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	refreshPhotoButton.Text = "REFRESH PHOTO"
	refreshPhotoButton.TextSize = 9
	refreshPhotoButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	refreshPhotoButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
	refreshPhotoButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	refreshPhotoButton.Parent = card
	corner(refreshPhotoButton,11)
	addStroke(refreshPhotoButton,accent,.9,.72)

	local photoRequest = 0
	local function refreshPhoto()
		photoRequest += 1
		local request = photoRequest
		setStatus("CREATOR PHOTO // LOADING")

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok,image = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
					creatorUserId,
					Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
					Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420
				)
			end)

			if request ~= photoRequest or not creatorPhoto.Parent then
				return
			end

			if ok and image then
				creatorPhoto.Image = image
				fallbackDragon.Visible = false
				setStatus("CREATOR PHOTO // READY")
			else
				fallbackDragon.Visible = true
				setStatus("CREATOR PHOTO // UNAVAILABLE")
			end
		end)
	end

	copyIdButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if type(setclipboard) == "function" then
			local ok = pcall(setclipboard,tostring(creatorUserId))
			setStatus(ok and "CREATOR ID COPIED" or "COPY FAILED")
		else
			setStatus("CLIPBOARD UNAVAILABLE // ID "..tostring(creatorUserId))
		end
	end)

	refreshPhotoButton.Activated:Connect(refreshPhoto)
	refreshPhoto()

	makeSection(
		creatorPage,
		"BUILD NOTES",
		"Every visible tool is available directly with no hidden gesture or access check."
	)
end
safeSetup("Creator",setupCreatorTab)


end

	local dashboardTab = makeTab("DASHBOARD","DASHBOARD")

	corner(dashCard,12)

		local function formatSession(seconds)
			seconds = math.max(0,math.floor(seconds))
			local minutes = math.floor(seconds/60)
			local hours = math.floor(minutes/60)
			minutes = minutes%60
			local secs = seconds%60
			if hours > 0 then
				return string.format("%dh %02dm %02ds",hours,minutes,secs)
			end
			return string.format("%dm %02ds",minutes,secs)
		end

		local function getPing()
			local value = "--"
			pcall(function()
				local stats = game:GetService("Stats")
				local network = stats:FindFirstChild("Network")
				local serverStats = network and network:FindFirstChild("ServerStatsItem")
				local pingItem = serverStats and serverStats:FindFirstChild("Data Ping")
				if pingItem then
					value = pingItem:GetValueString()
				end
			end)
			return value
		end

		local function updateServerSnapshot()
			if not serverDesc or not serverDesc.Parent then
				return
			end
			serverDesc.Text =
				"Players: "..tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
				.."  •  Ping: "..getPing()
				.."  •  Session: "..formatSession(os.clock()-startedAt)
		end

			local info =
				"NOT RUBY V1 | PlaceId: "..tostring(game.PlaceId)
				.." | JobId: "..tostring(game.JobId)
				.." | Players: "..tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())

			if type(setclipboard) == "function" then
				local ok = pcall(setclipboard,info)
				setStatus(ok and "SERVER INFO COPIED" or "COPY SERVER INFO FAILED")
			else
				setStatus("CLIPBOARD NOT SUPPORTED")
			end
		end)

		rejoinButton.Activated:Connect(function()
			setStatus("REJOINING SERVER...")
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok = pcall(function()
					local teleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
					if game.JobId ~= "" then
						teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(
							game.PlaceId,
							game.JobId,
							player
						)
					else
						teleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId,player)
					end
				end)
				if not ok then
					setStatus("REJOIN FAILED")
				end
			end)
		end)

				task.wait(1)

					updateServerSnapshot()
				end
			end
		end)
	end)()

			if type(setclipboard) == "function" then
				local ok = pcall(setclipboard,value)

			else

				camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom

				setStatus("CAMERA RESET")

	local liveStatus = makeSection(
		dashboardPage,
		"LIVE CHARACTER",
		"Waiting for character data..."
	)

	task.spawn(function()
		while not menuDeleted do
			task.wait(.35)
			if dashServer and dashServer.Parent then
				dashServer.Text = "PLAYERS: "..tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
			end
			if currentTab == "DASHBOARD" and liveStatus and liveStatus.Parent then
				local labels = {}
				for _,child in ipairs(liveStatus:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
						table.insert(labels,child)
					end
				end
				if #labels >= 2 then
					local health = humanoid and math.floor(humanoid.Health + .5) or 0
					local state = "UNKNOWN"

					if humanoid then
						local rawState = humanoid:GetState()
						local moving = humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > .05
						local onGround = humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air
						local verticalVelocity =
							root and root.Parent
							and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
							or 0

						if rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
							or (not onGround and verticalVelocity > 1) then
							state = "JUMPING"
						elseif rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
							or (not onGround and verticalVelocity <= 1) then
							state = "FALLING"
						elseif rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing then
							state = "CLIMBING"
						elseif rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
							state = "SWIMMING"
						elseif rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated then
							state = "SITTING"
						elseif moving and onGround then
							state = "WALKING"
						elseif onGround then
							state = "IDLE"
						else
							state = string.upper(rawState.Name)
						end
					end

					local pos = root and root.Parent and root.Position or Vector3.zero
					labels[2].Text = string.format(
						"HP: %d   |   State: %s   |   Position: %d, %d, %d",
						health,
						state,
						math.floor(pos.X),
						math.floor(pos.Y),
						math.floor(pos.Z)
					)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local refreshInspectButton,clearInspectButton =
		makePair(exploiterPage,"REFRESH","CLEAR PLAYER")

	makeSection(
		exploiterPage,
		"EXECUTOR LABELS",
		"Server labels recognized: Delta, Wave, Arceus X, Codex, Fluxus and Hydrogen. Other reported names are also shown."
	)

	local selectedPlayer = nil
	local lastPosition = nil
	local lastSampleTime = nil
	local airborneTime = 0
	local signalCounts = {SPEED = 0,TELEPORT = 0,AIR = 0,HOVER = 0,UPWARD = 0,PHYSICS = 0,FORCE = 0,JUMP = 0,NOCLIP = 0}
	local executorAliases = {
		delta = "DELTA",
		wave = "WAVE",
		arceus = "ARCEUS X",
		["arceus x"] = "ARCEUS X",
		codex = "CODEX",
		fluxus = "FLUXUS",
		hydrogen = "HYDROGEN"
	}

	local function normalizeExecutorName(value)
		if typeof(value) ~= "string" then
			return nil
		end
		local cleaned = string.gsub(value,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")
		if cleaned == "" then
			return nil
		end
		return executorAliases[string.lower(cleaned)] or string.upper(cleaned)
	end

	local function resetInspectorHistory()
		lastPosition = nil
		lastSampleTime = nil
		airborneTime = 0
		signalCounts.SPEED = 0
		signalCounts.TELEPORT = 0
		signalCounts.AIR = 0
		signalCounts.HOVER = 0
		signalCounts.UPWARD = 0
		signalCounts.PHYSICS = 0
		signalCounts.FORCE = 0
		signalCounts.JUMP = 0
		signalCounts.NOCLIP = 0
	end

	local function getGameWalkSpeedBaseline()
		local speeds = {}

		for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local otherCharacter = otherPlayer.Character
			local otherHumanoid =
				otherCharacter
				and otherCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if otherHumanoid and otherHumanoid.Health > 0 then
				table.insert(speeds,otherHumanoid.WalkSpeed)
			end
		end

		if #speeds == 0 then
			return 16
		end

		table.sort(speeds)

		-- Use the upper-middle server speed instead of assuming Roblox's
		-- default 16. If the game intentionally makes everybody faster,
		-- that becomes the detector's normal baseline.
		local index = math.clamp(
			math.ceil(#speeds * .75),
			1,
			#speeds
		)

		return math.max(16,speeds[index] or 16)
	end

	local function getGameJumpBaseline()
		local values = {}

		for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local otherCharacter = otherPlayer.Character
			local otherHumanoid =
				otherCharacter
				and otherCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if otherHumanoid and otherHumanoid.Health > 0 then
				local value =
					otherHumanoid.UseJumpPower
					and otherHumanoid.JumpPower
					or otherHumanoid.JumpHeight
				table.insert(values,value)
			end
		end

		if #values == 0 then
			return 50
		end

		table.sort(values)
		local index =
			math.clamp(math.ceil(#values*.75),1,#values)

		return values[index] or 50
	end

	local function hasCharacterForceObject(selectedCharacter)
		for _,object in ipairs(selectedCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("BodyVelocity")
				or object:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity")
				or object:IsA("BodyPosition")
				or object:IsA("BodyGyro")
				or object:IsA("LinearVelocity")
				or object:IsA("AngularVelocity")
				or object:IsA("VectorForce")
				or object:IsA("AlignPosition")
				or object:IsA("AlignOrientation") then
				return true,object.ClassName
			end
		end

		return false,nil
	end

	local function hasGroundBelow(selectedCharacter,selectedRoot,distance)
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {selectedCharacter}

		local result = workspace:Raycast(
			selectedRoot.Position,
			Vector3.new(0,-math.max(4,distance or 12),0),
			params
		)

		return result ~= nil
	end

	local function getSelectedParts()
		if not selectedPlayer or not selectedPlayer.Parent then
			return nil,nil,nil
		end
		local selectedCharacter = selectedPlayer.Character
		if not selectedCharacter then
			return nil,nil,nil
		end
		local selectedHumanoid = selectedCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local selectedRoot =
			selectedCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			or selectedCharacter.PrimaryPart
		return selectedCharacter,selectedHumanoid,selectedRoot
	end

	local function updateInspection()
		if not selectedPlayer or not selectedPlayer.Parent then
			selectedTitle.Text = "NO PLAYER SELECTED"
			selectedUser.Text = "--"
			inspectAvatar.Image = ""
			verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: --"
			verdictLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: --"
			executorLabel.TextColor3 = TEXT
			signalsLabel.Text = "SIGNALS: --"
			return
		end

		selectedTitle.Text = selectedPlayer.DisplayName
		selectedUser.Text = "@"..selectedPlayer.Name.."  •  "..tostring(selectedPlayer.UserId)

		local selectedCharacter,selectedHumanoid,selectedRoot = getSelectedParts()
		if not selectedCharacter or not selectedRoot then
			verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE"
			verdictLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: --"
			signalsLabel.Text = "SIGNALS: waiting for character"
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		if lastPosition and lastSampleTime then
			local dt = math.max(now - lastSampleTime,.001)
			local delta = selectedRoot.Position - lastPosition
			local horizontal =
				Vector3.new(delta.X,0,delta.Z).Magnitude / dt

			local gameWalkSpeed = getGameWalkSpeedBaseline()
			local playerWalkSpeed =
				selectedHumanoid
				and math.max(selectedHumanoid.WalkSpeed,0)
				or gameWalkSpeed

			-- GAME-SPEED-AWARE CHECK:
			-- Fast movement by itself is not enough anymore. The selected
			-- player's WalkSpeed must ALSO be a clear outlier compared with
			-- the rest of the server. This stops games with naturally high
			-- movement speed from being called suspicious.
			local walkSpeedOutlier =
				playerWalkSpeed > (
					strictBaseplateMode
					and math.max(
						gameWalkSpeed * 1.35,
						gameWalkSpeed + 8
					)
					or math.max(
						gameWalkSpeed * 1.65,
						gameWalkSpeed + 20
					)
				)

			local movementTooFast =
				horizontal > (
					strictBaseplateMode
					and math.max(
						gameWalkSpeed * 2.35,
						playerWalkSpeed * 1.7,
						52
					)
					or math.max(
						gameWalkSpeed * 3.5,
						playerWalkSpeed * 2.25,
						110
					)
				)

			if walkSpeedOutlier and movementTooFast then
				signalCounts.SPEED += 1
			else
				-- Old spikes slowly clear instead of permanently marking
				-- somebody suspicious after one network/game-speed burst.
				signalCounts.SPEED =
					math.max(0,signalCounts.SPEED - 1)
			end

			local teleportDistance =
				strictBaseplateMode and 75 or 180

			if delta.Magnitude > teleportDistance
				and dt < .65 then
				signalCounts.TELEPORT += 1
			end
			local velocity = selectedRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			local angularVelocity =
				selectedRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude

			-- Very high spin or velocity is a useful replicated physics signal.
			local physicsLinearThreshold =
				strictBaseplateMode and 95 or 165
			local physicsAngularThreshold =
				strictBaseplateMode and 18 or 42

			if velocity.Magnitude >= physicsLinearThreshold
				or angularVelocity >= physicsAngularThreshold then
				signalCounts.PHYSICS += 1
			else
				signalCounts.PHYSICS =
					math.max(0,signalCounts.PHYSICS - 1)
			end

			-- In a simple/baseplate game, character force objects are a useful
			-- extra clue. They are NOT treated as proof by themselves outside
			-- strict mode because legitimate games can also use constraints.
			local hasForceObject =
				hasCharacterForceObject(selectedCharacter)

			if hasForceObject then
				signalCounts.FORCE +=
					strictBaseplateMode and 1 or .25
			else
				signalCounts.FORCE =
					math.max(0,signalCounts.FORCE - .5)
			end

			if selectedHumanoid then
				local gameJump = getGameJumpBaseline()
				local selectedJump =
					selectedHumanoid.UseJumpPower
					and selectedHumanoid.JumpPower
					or selectedHumanoid.JumpHeight

				local jumpLimit =
					strictBaseplateMode
					and math.max(gameJump*1.35,gameJump+10)
					or math.max(gameJump*1.8,gameJump+30)

				if selectedJump > jumpLimit then
					signalCounts.JUMP += 1
				else
					signalCounts.JUMP =
						math.max(0,signalCounts.JUMP - 1)
				end
			end

			-- Strict mode checks whether the straight replicated movement path
			-- crossed a collidable object. Repeated hits can indicate noclip.
			if strictBaseplateMode and delta.Magnitude >= 4 then
				local pathParams = RaycastParams.new()
				pathParams.FilterType =
					Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				pathParams.FilterDescendantsInstances =
					{selectedCharacter}

				local pathHit = workspace:Raycast(
					lastPosition,
					delta,
					pathParams
				)

				if pathHit
					and pathHit.Instance
					and pathHit.Instance:IsA("BasePart")
					and pathHit.Instance.CanCollide then
					signalCounts.NOCLIP += 1
				else
					signalCounts.NOCLIP =
						math.max(0,signalCounts.NOCLIP - 1)
				end
			end

			local noGround =
				not hasGroundBelow(
					selectedCharacter,
					selectedRoot,
					12
				)

			local humanoidAir =
				selectedHumanoid
				and selectedHumanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air

			if humanoidAir and noGround then
				airborneTime += dt

				-- Hovering: clearly off the ground but barely falling/rising.
				if airborneTime >= (strictBaseplateMode and .75 or 1.35)
					and math.abs(velocity.Y) <= 5 then
					signalCounts.HOVER += 1
					airborneTime = .75
				end

				-- Controlled air travel: moving around while remaining far
				-- above any floor for long enough to rule out a normal jump.
				local horizontalAir =
					Vector3.new(
						velocity.X,
						0,
						velocity.Z
					).Magnitude

				if airborneTime >= (strictBaseplateMode and 1.25 or 2.25)
					and horizontalAir >= 5 then
					signalCounts.AIR += 1
					airborneTime = 1
				end

				-- Repeated/controlled upward travel while there is no nearby
				-- floor. A normal jump may spike once, so require repeats.
				if velocity.Y >= (strictBaseplateMode and 12 or 18) then
					signalCounts.UPWARD += 1
				else
					signalCounts.UPWARD =
						math.max(0,signalCounts.UPWARD - 1)
				end
			else
				airborneTime = 0
				signalCounts.UPWARD =
					math.max(0,signalCounts.UPWARD - 1)
			end
		end

		lastPosition = selectedRoot.Position
		lastSampleTime = now

		local signals = {}
		if signalCounts.SPEED >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"abnormal speed")
		end
		if signalCounts.TELEPORT >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"repeated position jumps")
		end
		if signalCounts.AIR >= 1 then
			table.insert(signals,"controlled airborne travel")
		end
		if signalCounts.HOVER >= 1 then
			table.insert(signals,"hovering with no ground below")
		end
		if signalCounts.UPWARD >= 3 then
			table.insert(signals,"repeated upward flight-like movement")
		end
		if signalCounts.PHYSICS >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"extreme replicated physics spike")
		end
		if signalCounts.FORCE >= (strictBaseplateMode and 2 or 5) then
			table.insert(signals,"character force / mover object")
		end
		if signalCounts.JUMP >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"abnormal jump setting")
		end
		if strictBaseplateMode and signalCounts.NOCLIP >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"possible noclip through solid parts")
		end

		local serverFlag =
			selectedPlayer:GetAttribute("ExploitDetected") == true
		local serverReason =
			selectedPlayer:GetAttribute("ExploitReason")
		local executorName = normalizeExecutorName(
			selectedPlayer:GetAttribute("ExecutorName")
		)

		local speedOnlyServerFlag = false
		if serverFlag
			and typeof(serverReason) == "string"
			and serverReason ~= "" then
			local loweredReason = string.lower(serverReason)
			speedOnlyServerFlag =
				string.find(loweredReason,"speed",1,true) ~= nil
				and string.find(loweredReason,"flight",1,true) == nil
				and string.find(loweredReason,"air",1,true) == nil
				and string.find(loweredReason,"teleport",1,true) == nil
		end

		if serverFlag and not speedOnlyServerFlag then
			table.insert(signals,"server anti-cheat flag")
		elseif speedOnlyServerFlag then
			table.insert(signals,"fast movement (may be game speed)")
		end

		if typeof(serverReason) == "string"
			and serverReason ~= ""
			and not speedOnlyServerFlag then
			table.insert(signals,"server: "..serverReason)
		end

		local strongLocalSignal =
			signalCounts.TELEPORT >= 2
			or signalCounts.AIR >= 1
			or signalCounts.HOVER >= 1
			or signalCounts.UPWARD >= 3
			or signalCounts.SPEED >= 2
			or signalCounts.PHYSICS >= 2
			or signalCounts.JUMP >= 2
			or (
				strictBaseplateMode
				and (
					signalCounts.FORCE >= 2
					or signalCounts.NOCLIP >= 2
				)
			)

		local suspicious =
			(serverFlag and not speedOnlyServerFlag)
			or executorName ~= nil
			or strongLocalSignal

		if suspicious then
			verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: SUSPICIOUS"
			verdictLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,92,108)
		else
			verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: NONE DETECTED"
			verdictLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90,230,135)
		end

		if executorName then
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: "..executorName
			executorLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,92,108)
		elseif suspicious then
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: UNKNOWN"
			executorLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,170,95)
		else
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: NONE"
			executorLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90,230,135)
		end

		if #signals == 0 then
			signalsLabel.Text =
				"SIGNALS: none observed. This detects replicated behavior/character anomalies, not hidden script code. Exact executor names still require server-reported ExecutorName."
		else
			signalsLabel.Text = "SIGNALS: "..table.concat(signals,", ")
		end
	end

	local function selectInspectorPlayer(otherPlayer)
		if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == player then
			return
		end

		selectedPlayer = otherPlayer
		resetInspectorHistory()

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok,image = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
					otherPlayer.UserId,
					Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
					Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
				)
			end)
			if ok and image and inspectAvatar.Parent and selectedPlayer == otherPlayer then
				inspectAvatar.Image = image
			end
		end)

		updateInspection()
		setStatus("INSPECTING @"..otherPlayer.Name)
	end

	selectPlayerButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		showPlayerPicker("SELECT PLAYER TO INSPECT",selectInspectorPlayer,false)
	end)

	refreshInspectButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		resetInspectorHistory()
		updateInspection()
		setStatus(selectedPlayer and "INSPECTION REFRESHED" or "SELECT A PLAYER FIRST")
	end)

	clearInspectButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		selectedPlayer = nil
		resetInspectorHistory()
		updateInspection()
		setStatus("INSPECTION CLEARED")
	end)

	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(leavingPlayer)
		if selectedPlayer == leavingPlayer then
			selectedPlayer = nil
			resetInspectorHistory()
			updateInspection()
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while not menuDeleted do
			task.wait(.25)
			if selectedPlayer and currentTab == "EXPLOITER" and exploiterPage.Parent then
				updateInspection()
			end
		end
	end)
end
safeSetup("Dashboard",setupFinalAdminTabs)

end
local function setToggle(button,on,label)
	if not button or not button.Parent then
		return
	end
	activeToggleButtons[button] = {
		on = on,
		label = label
	}
	if on then
		button.BackgroundColor3 = accent
		button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
		button.Text = label.."  [ON]"
	else
		button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		button.TextColor3 = TEXT
		button.Text = label.."  [OFF]"
	end
end
local function refreshToggleColors()
	for button,data in pairs(activeToggleButtons) do
		if button and button.Parent then
			setToggle(button,data.on,data.label)
		end
	end
end

local function colorsMatch(first,second)
	return first ~= nil
		and second ~= nil
		and math.abs(first.R-second.R) < .001
		and math.abs(first.G-second.G) < .001
		and math.abs(first.B-second.B) < .001
end

local function refreshAccentBoundVisuals(previousAccent)
	if not previousAccent then
		return
	end

	for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("GuiObject")
			and colorsMatch(object.BackgroundColor3,previousAccent) then
			object.BackgroundColor3 = accent
		end

		if object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton")
			or object:IsA("TextBox") then
			local baseColor =
				object:GetAttribute("NotRubyBaseTextColor")
			if baseColor ~= nil
				and colorsMatch(baseColor,previousAccent) then
				object:SetAttribute("NotRubyBaseTextColor",accent)
			end
		end

		if (object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton")
			or object:IsA("TextBox"))
			and colorsMatch(object.TextColor3,previousAccent) then
			object.TextColor3 = accent
		end

		if (object:IsA("ImageLabel") or object:IsA("ImageButton"))
			and colorsMatch(object.ImageColor3,previousAccent) then
			object.ImageColor3 = accent
		end

		if object:IsA("ScrollingFrame")
			and colorsMatch(object.ScrollBarImageColor3,previousAccent) then
			object.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent
		end

		if object:IsA("UIGradient") then
			local changed = false
			local keypoints = {}
			for _,keypoint in ipairs(object.Color.Keypoints) do
				local value = keypoint.Value
				if colorsMatch(value,previousAccent) then
					value = accent
					changed = true
				end
				table.insert(
					keypoints,
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(keypoint.Time,value)
				)
			end
			if changed then
				object.Color = ColorSequence.new(keypoints)
			end
		end
	end

	local aura =
		player.Character
		and player.Character:FindFirstChild("NotRubySelfAura",true)
	if aura and aura:IsA("Highlight")
		and colorsMatch(aura.FillColor,previousAccent) then
		aura.FillColor = accent
	end
end

local function cycleTheme()
	local previousAccent = accent
	themeIndex += 1
	if themeIndex > #themes then
		themeIndex = 1
	end

	accent = themes[themeIndex].color
	refreshAccentBoundVisuals(previousAccent)
	status.TextColor3 = accent
	sidebar.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent

	applyTabVisuals()
	refreshToggleColors()

	-- MATCH makes every outline follow the selected theme.
	if gui:GetAttribute("OutlineMode") == "MATCH" then
		gui:SetAttribute("OutlineColor",accent)
		for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("UIStroke")
				and object:GetAttribute("NotRubyFixedColor") ~= true then
				object.Color = accent
			end
		end
	end

	setStatus("THEME: "..themes[themeIndex].name)
end

-- UI LAB // visual quality + mobile fit controls
;(function()
	makeSection(
		settingsPage,
		"UI LAB",
		"Extra menu-only effects and layout tools. These do not change gameplay."
	)

	local blurButton,pulseButton =
		makePair(
			settingsPage,
			"BACKGROUND BLUR [OFF]",
			"HEADER PULSE [ON]"
		)

	local fitButton,resetFxButton =
		makePair(
			settingsPage,
			"FIT TO SCREEN",
			"RESET UI EFFECTS"
		)

	local uiScaleButton,autoFitButton =
		makePair(
			settingsPage,
			"UI SCALE: 100%",
			"AUTO FIT [OFF]"
		)

	local oldBlur = Lighting:FindFirstChild("NotRubyMenuBlur")
	if oldBlur then
		oldBlur:Destroy()
	end

	local blurEffect = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
	blurEffect.Name = "NotRubyMenuBlur"
	blurEffect.Size = 0
	blurEffect.Parent = Lighting

	local blurEnabled = false
	local pulseEnabled = true
	local autoFitEnabled = false
	local uiScaleValues = {.85,1,1.15}
	local uiScaleIndex = 2

	local function setBlur(value)
		blurEnabled = value == true
		setToggle(blurButton,blurEnabled,"BACKGROUND BLUR")
		TweenService:Create(
			blurEffect,
			TweenInfo.new(.22,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{Size = blurEnabled and 14 or 0}
		):Play()
		setStatus(blurEnabled and "BACKGROUND BLUR ON" or "BACKGROUND BLUR OFF")
	end

	local function setHeaderPulse(value)
		pulseEnabled = value == true
		setToggle(pulseButton,pulseEnabled,"HEADER PULSE")
		local glow = header:FindFirstChild("AnimatedAccentGlow")
		if glow then
			glow.Visible = pulseEnabled
		end
		setStatus(pulseEnabled and "HEADER PULSE ON" or "HEADER PULSE OFF")
	end

	local function fitToScreen()
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport =
			camera and camera.ViewportSize
			or Vector2.new(800,600)

		local width =
			math.max(340,math.min(620,viewport.X-24))
		local height =
			math.max(300,math.min(460,viewport.Y-44))

		panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width,height)
		panel.Position = UDim2.new(.5,-width*.5,.5,-height*.5)
		setPanelScale(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale"),false)
		setStatus("UI FIT TO SCREEN")
	end

	local function applyUiScale()
		local value = uiScaleValues[uiScaleIndex] or 1
		setPanelScale(value,true)
		uiScaleButton.Text =
			"UI SCALE: "..tostring(math.floor(value*100+.5)).."%"
		setStatus(uiScaleButton.Text)
	end

	local function setAutoFit(value)
		autoFitEnabled = value == true
		setToggle(autoFitButton,autoFitEnabled,"AUTO FIT")
		if autoFitEnabled then
			fitToScreen()
		end
		setStatus(autoFitEnabled and "AUTO FIT ON" or "AUTO FIT OFF")
	end

	blurButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setBlur(not blurEnabled)
	end)

	pulseButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setHeaderPulse(not pulseEnabled)
	end)

	fitButton.Activated:Connect(fitToScreen)

	uiScaleButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local current =
			tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale")) or 1
		uiScaleIndex = 1
		for index,value in ipairs(uiScaleValues) do
			if value > current+.001 then
				uiScaleIndex = index
				break
			end
		end
		applyUiScale()
	end)

	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("PanelScale"):Connect(function()
		local value =
			tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale")) or 1
		uiScaleButton.Text =
			"UI SCALE: "..tostring(math.floor(value*100+.5)).."%"
	end)

	autoFitButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setAutoFit(not autoFitEnabled)
	end)

	resetFxButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setBlur(false)
		setHeaderPulse(true)
		autoFitEnabled = false
		setToggle(autoFitButton,false,"AUTO FIT")
		uiScaleIndex = 2
		applyUiScale()
		setStatus("UI EFFECTS RESET")
	end)

	setToggle(blurButton,false,"BACKGROUND BLUR")
	setToggle(pulseButton,true,"HEADER PULSE")
	setToggle(autoFitButton,false,"AUTO FIT")

	task.spawn(function()
		local lastViewport = Vector2.new(0,0)
		while gui.Parent and not menuDeleted do
			task.wait(.5)
			if autoFitEnabled then
				camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
				local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize
				if viewport and viewport ~= lastViewport then
					lastViewport = viewport
					fitToScreen()
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	gui.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
		if not parent and blurEffect and blurEffect.Parent then
			blurEffect:Destroy()
		end
	end)
end)()

local function applyMovementStats()
	if not humanoid then
		return
	end
	humanoid.WalkSpeed =
		sprinting and math.max(walkSpeed,sprintSpeed) or walkSpeed
	pcall(function()
		humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
		humanoid.JumpPower = jumpPower
	end)
end
walkApply.Activated:Connect(function()
	local value = tonumber(walkBox.Text)
	if not value then
		setStatus("INVALID WALK SPEED")
		return
	end
	walkSpeed = math.clamp(value,1,250)
	applyMovementStats()
	setStatus("WALK "..tostring(walkSpeed))
end)
jumpApply.Activated:Connect(function()
	local value = tonumber(jumpBox.Text)
	if not value then
		setStatus("INVALID JUMP POWER")
		return
	end
	jumpPower = math.clamp(value,0,250)
	applyMovementStats()
	setStatus("JUMP "..tostring(jumpPower))
end)
sprintApply.Activated:Connect(function()
	local value = tonumber(sprintBox.Text)
	if not value then
		setStatus("INVALID SPRINT SPEED")
		return
	end
	sprintSpeed = math.clamp(value,1,300)
	applyMovementStats()
	setStatus("SPRINT SPEED "..tostring(sprintSpeed))
end)
flyApply.Activated:Connect(function()

		return

end)

		setStatus("INVALID SCAN RADIUS")

end)
