local upButton = makeFlightHold("UP",0)
local downButton = makeFlightHold("DOWN",76)
local function holdButton(button,callback)
	button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			callback(true)
			button.BackgroundColor3 = accent
			button.TextColor3 = ACTIVE_TEXT
		end
	end)
	button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			callback(false)
			button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
			button.TextColor3 = TEXT
		end
	end)
end
holdButton(upButton,function(value)
	flyUp = value
end)
holdButton(downButton,function(value)
	flyDown = value
end)
local function stopFlight()
	flying = false
	flyUp = false
	flyDown = false
	if bodyGyro then
		bodyGyro:Destroy()
		bodyGyro = nil
	end
	if flightVelocity then
		flightVelocity:Destroy()
		flightVelocity = nil
	end
	if root and root.Parent then
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end
	if humanoid then
		humanoid.AutoRotate = true
	end
	if flightControls and flightControls.Parent then
		flightControls.Visible = false
	end
	if flightButton and flightButton.Parent then
		setToggle(flightButton,false,"FREE FLIGHT")
	end
	if quickFlight and quickFlight.Parent then
		setToggle(quickFlight,false,"FREE FLIGHT")
	end
end
local function startFlight()
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if not character or not character.Parent then
		loadCharacter()
	end
	if not root or not root.Parent or not humanoid then
		return
	end
	if bodyGyro then
		bodyGyro:Destroy()
	end
	if flightVelocity then
		flightVelocity:Destroy()
	end
	root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	bodyGyro.Name = "ClarkFreeFlightGyro"
	bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e8,1e8,1e8)
	bodyGyro.P = 60000
	bodyGyro.D = 800
	bodyGyro.CFrame = root.CFrame
	bodyGyro.Parent = root
	flightVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
	flightVelocity.Name = "ClarkFreeFlightVelocity"
	flightVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9,1e9,1e9)
	flightVelocity.P = 30000
	flightVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
	flightVelocity.Parent = root
	humanoid.AutoRotate = false
	flying = true
	flightControls.Visible = true
	setToggle(flightButton,true,"FREE FLIGHT")
end
local function setFlight(value)
	if value then
		startFlight()
	else
		stopFlight()
	end
	setToggle(quickFlight,flying,"FREE FLIGHT")
	setStatus(flying and "FREE FLIGHT ON" or "FREE FLIGHT OFF")
end
local function setPitchFlight(value)
	pitchFlight = value
	setToggle(pitchButton,pitchFlight,"PITCH FLIGHT")
	setStatus(pitchFlight and "CAMERA PITCH FLIGHT ON" or "LEVEL FLIGHT ON")
end
flightButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setFlight(not flying)
end)
pitchButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setPitchFlight(not pitchFlight)
end)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if not flying
		or not root
		or not root.Parent
		or not humanoid
		or not bodyGyro
		or not flightVelocity then
		return
	end
	camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then
		return
	end
	local direction = Vector3.zero
	local keyboardUsed = false
	local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
	local right = camera.CFrame.RightVector
	if not pitchFlight then
		look = Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)
		right = Vector3.new(right.X,0,right.Z)
		if look.Magnitude > .001 then
			look = look.Unit
		end
		if right.Magnitude > .001 then
			right = right.Unit
		end
	end
	if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
		direction += look
		keyboardUsed = true
	end
	if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
		direction -= look
		keyboardUsed = true
	end
	if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
		direction += right
		keyboardUsed = true
	end
	if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
		direction -= right
		keyboardUsed = true
	end
	if not keyboardUsed and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > .03 then
		local flatLook = Vector3.new(
			camera.CFrame.LookVector.X,
			0,
			camera.CFrame.LookVector.Z
		)
		local flatRight = Vector3.new(
			camera.CFrame.RightVector.X,
			0,
			camera.CFrame.RightVector.Z
		)
		if flatLook.Magnitude > .001 then
			flatLook = flatLook.Unit
		end
		if flatRight.Magnitude > .001 then
			flatRight = flatRight.Unit
		end
		local move = humanoid.MoveDirection
		local forwardAmount = move:Dot(flatLook)
		local rightAmount = move:Dot(flatRight)
		local mobileLook =
			pitchFlight and camera.CFrame.LookVector or flatLook
		local mobileRight =
			pitchFlight and camera.CFrame.RightVector or flatRight
		direction += mobileLook * forwardAmount
		direction += mobileRight * rightAmount
	end
	local vertical = 0
	if flyUp then
		vertical += 1
	end
	if flyDown then
		vertical -= 1
	end
	if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)
		or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then
		vertical += 1
	end
	if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)
		or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then
		vertical -= 1
	end
	direction += Vector3.new(0,vertical,0)
	if direction.Magnitude > 1 then
		direction = direction.Unit
	end
	flightVelocity.Velocity = direction * flySpeed
	local facing = camera.CFrame.LookVector
	local flatFacing = Vector3.new(facing.X,0,facing.Z)
	if flatFacing.Magnitude > .01 then
		bodyGyro.CFrame =
			CFrame.lookAt(
				root.Position,
				root.Position + flatFacing.Unit
			)
	end
end)
local function setSprint(value)
	sprinting = value
	applyMovementStats()
	setToggle(sprintButton,sprinting,"SPRINT")
	setStatus(sprinting and "SPRINT ON" or "SPRINT OFF")
end
sprintButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setSprint(not sprinting)
end)
local function restoreCollision()
	for part,oldValue in pairs(originalCollision) do
		if part and part.Parent then
			part.CanCollide = oldValue
		end
	end
	table.clear(originalCollision)
end
local function setNoclip(value)
	noclip = value
	setToggle(noclipButton,noclip,"NOCLIP")
	setToggle(quickNoclip,noclip,"NOCLIP")
	if not noclip then
		restoreCollision()
	end
	setStatus(noclip and "NOCLIP ON" or "NOCLIP OFF")
end
noclipButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setNoclip(not noclip)
end)
RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if not noclip or not character then
		return
	end
	for _,object in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("BasePart") then
			if originalCollision[object] == nil then
				originalCollision[object] = object.CanCollide
			end
			object.CanCollide = false
		end
	end
end)
resetMovementButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setFlight(false)
	setSprint(false)
	setNoclip(false)
	walkSpeed = 16
	jumpPower = 50
	sprintSpeed = 42
	flySpeed = 70
	walkBox.Text = "16"
	jumpBox.Text = "50"
	sprintBox.Text = "42"
	flyBox.Text = "70"
	applyMovementStats()
	setStatus("MOVEMENT RESET")
end)
local function applyLightingProfile()
	local normalized = (brightnessLevel-50)/50
	local baseBrightness = originalLighting.Brightness
	local baseExposure = originalLighting.ExposureCompensation

	if fullBright then
		baseBrightness = math.max(baseBrightness,3)
		baseExposure = math.max(baseExposure,.35)
		Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(180,180,180)
		Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(180,180,180)
	else
		Lighting.Ambient = originalLighting.Ambient
		Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = originalLighting.OutdoorAmbient
	end

	Lighting.Brightness =
		math.clamp(baseBrightness+normalized*2.2,0,10)
	Lighting.ExposureCompensation =
		math.clamp(baseExposure+normalized*1.8,-3,3)
end

local function setNightVision(value)
	nightVision = value == true
	setToggle(nightButton,nightVision,"NIGHT VISION")

	if visionEffect then
		visionEffect:Destroy()
		visionEffect = nil
	end

	if nightVision then
		visionEffect = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
		visionEffect.Name = "NotRubyNightVision"
		visionEffect.Brightness = .18
		visionEffect.Contrast = .12
		visionEffect.Saturation = -.05
		visionEffect.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(165,255,185)
		visionEffect.Parent = Lighting
	end

	setStatus(nightVision and "NIGHT VISION ON" or "NIGHT VISION OFF")
end

nightButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setNightVision(not nightVision)
end)

local function setFullBright(value)
	fullBright = value == true
	setToggle(brightButton,fullBright,"FULL BRIGHT")
	applyLightingProfile()
	setStatus(fullBright and "FULL BRIGHT ON" or "FULL BRIGHT OFF")
end

brightButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setFullBright(not fullBright)
end)

local function setBrightnessLevel(value)
	value = tonumber(value)
	if not value then
		setStatus("INVALID BRIGHTNESS")
		return false
	end

	brightnessLevel = math.clamp(math.floor(value+.5),0,100)
	brightnessBox.Text = tostring(brightnessLevel)
	applyLightingProfile()
	setStatus("BRIGHTNESS "..tostring(brightnessLevel).."/100")
	return true
end

brightnessApply.Activated:Connect(function()
	setBrightnessLevel(brightnessBox.Text)
end)
local function applyXrayPart(object)
	if not xrayMode
		or not object:IsA("BasePart")
		or not character
		or object:IsDescendantOf(character)
		or object.Transparency >= 1 then
		return
	end
	if originalTransparency[object] == nil then
		originalTransparency[object] = object.LocalTransparencyModifier
	end
	object.LocalTransparencyModifier = .65
end
local function restoreXray()
	for object,oldValue in pairs(originalTransparency) do
		if object and object.Parent then
			object.LocalTransparencyModifier = oldValue
		end
	end
	table.clear(originalTransparency)
end
local function setXray(value)
	xrayMode = value
	setToggle(xrayButton,xrayMode,"X-RAY")
	if xrayMode then
		for _,object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			applyXrayPart(object)
		end
	else
		restoreXray()
	end
	setStatus(xrayMode and "X-RAY ON" or "X-RAY OFF")
end
xrayButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setXray(not xrayMode)
end)
workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if xrayMode and object:IsA("BasePart") then
		task.defer(function()
			applyXrayPart(object)
		end)
	end
end)

-- WALL / COLLISION PARTS
-- Shows nearby collidable geometry with red outlines. This is useful for
-- seeing actual solid surfaces instead of decorative/non-collidable parts.
gui:SetAttribute("WallPartsVisual",false)

do
local function setupWallPartsVisualizer()
	local scanRadius = 120
	local refreshDelay = .45
	local visualFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	visualFolder.Name = "NotRubyWallPartVisuals"
	visualFolder.Parent = workspace
	local activeBoxes = {}
	local token = 0

	local function isCharacterPart(part)
		local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		return model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") ~= nil
	end

	local function clearBoxes()
		for part,box in pairs(activeBoxes) do
			if box then
				box:Destroy()
			end
			activeBoxes[part] = nil
		end
	end

	local function removeMissingParts(seen)
		for part,box in pairs(activeBoxes) do
			if not seen[part] or not part.Parent then
				if box then
					box:Destroy()
				end
				activeBoxes[part] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	local function getPartVisualColor(part)
		local smallest =
			math.min(part.Size.X,part.Size.Y,part.Size.Z)

		if smallest <= 1.15 then
			return Color3.fromRGB(255,205,55)
		end

		if part.CanCollide then
			return Color3.fromRGB(255,45,60)
		end

		return Color3.fromRGB(155,160,175)
	end

	local function addPart(part)
		if activeBoxes[part]
			or not part:IsA("BasePart")
			or part.Transparency >= 1
			or isCharacterPart(part) then
			return
		end

		local color = getPartVisualColor(part)
		local box = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
		box.Name = "NotRubyCollisionOutline"
		box.Adornee = part
		box.Color3 = color
		box.SurfaceColor3 = color
		box.SurfaceTransparency = .93
		box.LineThickness = .035
		box.Parent = visualFolder
		activeBoxes[part] = box
	end

	local function refresh()
		local currentRoot =
			player.Character
			and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not currentRoot then
			clearBoxes()
			return
		end

		local overlap = OverlapParams.new()
		overlap.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		overlap.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
			player.Character,
			visualFolder
		}

		local seen = {}
		local nearby = workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(
			currentRoot.Position,
			scanRadius,
			overlap
		)

		for _,part in ipairs(nearby) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart")
				and part.Transparency < 1
				and not isCharacterPart(part) then
				seen[part] = true

				local existing = activeBoxes[part]
				if existing then
					local color = getPartVisualColor(part)
					existing.Color3 = color
					existing.SurfaceColor3 = color
				else
					addPart(part)
				end
			end
		end

		removeMissingParts(seen)
	end

	local function stop()
		token += 1
		clearBoxes()
		setToggle(
			wallPartsButton,
			false,
			"WALL / COLLISION PARTS"
		)
	end

	local function start()
		token += 1
		local myToken = token
		setToggle(
			wallPartsButton,
			true,
			"WALL / COLLISION PARTS"
		)

		task.spawn(function()
			while not menuDeleted
				and gui:GetAttribute("WallPartsVisual") == true
				and myToken == token do
				refresh()
				task.wait(refreshDelay)
			end

			if myToken == token then
				clearBoxes()
			end
		end)
	end

	wallPartsButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		gui:SetAttribute(
			"WallPartsVisual",
			not (gui:GetAttribute("WallPartsVisual") == true)
		)
	end)

	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("WallPartsVisual"):Connect(function()
		if gui:GetAttribute("WallPartsVisual") == true then
			start()
			setStatus("WALL PARTS // RED SOLID // GRAY NON-COLLIDE // YELLOW THIN")
		else
			stop()
			setStatus("WALL / COLLISION PARTS OFF")
		end
	end)

	gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
		clearBoxes()
		if visualFolder then
			visualFolder:Destroy()
		end
	end)
end
safeSetup("Wall Visuals",setupWallPartsVisualizer)

end
fovApply.Activated:Connect(function()
	local value = tonumber(fovBox.Text)
	if not value then
		setStatus("INVALID FOV")
		return
	end
	value = math.clamp(value,40,120)
	camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		camera.FieldOfView = value
	end
	fovBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(value))
	setStatus("FOV "..tostring(value))
end)
fovResetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		camera.FieldOfView = originalFOV
	end
	fovBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(originalFOV))
	setStatus("FOV RESET")
end)
local function resetVisuals()
	setNightVision(false)
	brightnessLevel = 50
	brightnessBox.Text = "50"
	setFullBright(false)
	applyLightingProfile()
	setXray(false)
	gui:SetAttribute("WallPartsVisual",false)
	camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		camera.FieldOfView = originalFOV
	end
	fovBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(originalFOV))
	setStatus("VISUALS RESET")
end
resetVisualsButton.Activated:Connect(resetVisuals)
local function getRoot(model)
	if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then
		return nil
	end
	local preferred = {
		"HumanoidRootPart",
		"UpperTorso",
		"Torso",
		"Head"
	}
	for _,name in ipairs(preferred) do
		local part = model:FindFirstChild(name)
		if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
			return part
		end
	end
	for _,object in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
		if object:IsA("BasePart") then
			return object
		end
	end
	return nil
end
local function removeMarker(markerTable,target)
	local data = markerTable[target]
	if data then
		if data.gui then
			data.gui:Destroy()
		end
		markerTable[target] = nil
	end
end
local function clearMarkerTable(markerTable)
	for _,data in pairs(markerTable) do
		if data.gui then
			data.gui:Destroy()
		end
	end
	table.clear(markerTable)
end
local function createMarker(target,name,isMonster)
	if not target or not target:IsA("Model") then
		return
	end
	local targetRoot = getRoot(target)
	if not targetRoot then
		return
	end
	if root and (root.Position-targetRoot.Position).Magnitude > scanRadius then
		return
	end
	local markerTable = isMonster and monsterMarkers or playerMarkers
	if markerTable[target] then
		return
	end
	local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	billboard.Name = isMonster and "ClarkMonsterMarker" or "ClarkPlayerMarker"
	billboard.Adornee = targetRoot
	billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(245,72)
	billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,4,0)
	billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
	billboard.MaxDistance = scanRadius
	billboard.Parent = gui
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.Parent = billboard
	local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,27)
	nameLabel.Text = isMonster and "MONSTER // "..name or name
	nameLabel.TextSize = 12
	nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	nameLabel.TextColor3 =
		isMonster
		and Color3.fromRGB(255,68,78)
		or Color3.fromRGB(72,240,125)
	nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = .15
	nameLabel.Parent = frame
	local distanceLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	distanceLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	distanceLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,27)
	distanceLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,20)
	distanceLabel.Text = "DISTANCE: --"
	distanceLabel.TextSize = 10
	distanceLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	distanceLabel.TextColor3 = TEXT
	distanceLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = .2
	distanceLabel.Parent = frame
	markerTable[target] = {
		gui = billboard,
		distance = distanceLabel
	}
end
local function refreshPlayers()
	clearMarkerTable(playerMarkers)
	if not playerLocator then
		return
	end

	local selectedUserId = tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("TrackerTargetUserId")) or 0
	local selectedName = tostring(gui:GetAttribute("TrackerTargetName") or "")
	local foundTarget = false
	local markedCount = 0

	for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if otherPlayer ~= player
			and otherPlayer.Character
			and (
				selectedUserId == 0
				or otherPlayer.UserId == selectedUserId
			) then

			createMarker(
				otherPlayer.Character,
				otherPlayer.DisplayName.."  @"..otherPlayer.Name,
				false
			)

			markedCount += 1

			if selectedUserId ~= 0 and otherPlayer.UserId == selectedUserId then
				foundTarget = true
			end
		end
	end

	if trackerInfo and trackerInfo.Parent then
		local labels = {}
		for _,child in ipairs(trackerInfo:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
				table.insert(labels,child)
			end
		end

		if #labels >= 2 then
			if selectedUserId == 0 then
				labels[2].Text =
					"Tracking all players  |  Marked: "..tostring(markedCount)
			elseif foundTarget then
				labels[2].Text =
					"Tracking only @"..selectedName
			else
				labels[2].Text =
					"Selected player is not currently available"
			end
		end
	end
end
local function setPlayerLocator(value)
	playerLocator = value
	setToggle(playersButton,playerLocator,"PLAYER LOCATOR")
	setToggle(quickPlayers,playerLocator,"PLAYER LOCATOR")
	if playerLocator then
		refreshPlayers()
	else
		clearMarkerTable(playerMarkers)
	end
	setStatus(playerLocator and "PLAYER LOCATOR ON" or "PLAYER LOCATOR OFF")
end
playersButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setPlayerLocator(not playerLocator)
end)
refreshPlayersButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	if playerLocator then
		refreshPlayers()
		setStatus("PLAYERS REFRESHED")
	else
		setStatus("PLAYER LOCATOR OFF")
	end
end)

do
local function setupPlayerTrackerSelector()
	gui:SetAttribute("TrackerTargetUserId",0)
	gui:SetAttribute("TrackerTargetName","")

	makeSection(
		trackersPage,
		"TARGET MODE",
		"Track everyone, or choose one player from a server list with profile photos."
	)

	local selectTargetButton,allPlayersButton =
		makePair(trackersPage,"SELECT PLAYER","ALL PLAYERS")

	local selectedCard = Instance.new("Frame")
	selectedCard.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,82)
	selectedCard.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	selectedCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
	selectedCard.Parent = trackersPage
	corner(selectedCard,11)

	local selectedAvatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	selectedAvatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58,58)
	selectedAvatar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10,12)
	selectedAvatar.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	selectedAvatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	selectedAvatar.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
	selectedAvatar.Parent = selectedCard
	corner(selectedAvatar,29)
	addStroke(selectedAvatar,accent,1.2,.1)

	local selectedName = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	selectedName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selectedName.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(80,15)
	selectedName.Size = UDim2.new(1,-92,0,22)
	selectedName.Text = "TARGET: ALL PLAYERS"
	selectedName.TextSize = 10
	selectedName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	selectedName.TextColor3 = TEXT
	selectedName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	selectedName.Parent = selectedCard

	local selectedUser = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	selectedUser.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selectedUser.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(80,40)
	selectedUser.Size = UDim2.new(1,-92,0,20)
	selectedUser.Text = "Select a player to track only them."
	selectedUser.TextSize = 9
	selectedUser.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	selectedUser.TextColor3 = MUTED
	selectedUser.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	selectedUser.Parent = selectedCard

	local function setAllPlayers()
		gui:SetAttribute("TrackerTargetUserId",0)
		gui:SetAttribute("TrackerTargetName","")
		selectedAvatar.Image = ""
		selectedName.Text = "TARGET: ALL PLAYERS"
		selectedUser.Text = "Locator will mark every other player."
		if playerLocator then
			refreshPlayers()
		end
		setStatus("TRACKING ALL PLAYERS")
	end

	local function selectPlayer(otherPlayer)
		if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == player then
			return
		end

		gui:SetAttribute("TrackerTargetUserId",otherPlayer.UserId)
		gui:SetAttribute("TrackerTargetName",otherPlayer.Name)
		selectedName.Text = otherPlayer.DisplayName
		selectedUser.Text = "@"..otherPlayer.Name.."  •  TRACK ONLY THIS PLAYER"

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok,image = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
					otherPlayer.UserId,
					Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
					Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
				)
			end)
			if ok and image and selectedAvatar.Parent
				and tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("TrackerTargetUserId")) == otherPlayer.UserId then
				selectedAvatar.Image = image
			end
		end)

		if playerLocator then
			refreshPlayers()
		end
		setStatus("TRACKING @"..otherPlayer.Name)
	end

	selectTargetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		showPlayerPicker("SELECT PLAYER TO LOCATE",selectPlayer,false)
	end)

	allPlayersButton.Activated:Connect(setAllPlayers)

	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(leavingPlayer)
		local selectedId = tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("TrackerTargetUserId")) or 0
		if selectedId == leavingPlayer.UserId then
			setAllPlayers()
		end
	end)
end
safeSetup("Player Selector",setupPlayerTrackerSelector)

end
local monsterWords = {
	"locust","monster","zombie","creature","beast","enemy",
	"alien","mutant","boss","npc","insect","bug","spider",
	"scorpion","demon","ghoul","crawler","creeper","behemoth",
	"titan","parasite","beetle","wasp","hornet"
}
local function normalizeName(name)
	return string.lower(
		string.gsub(name or "","[%s_%-%p]+"," ")
	)
end
local function containsMonsterWord(name)
	name = normalizeName(name)
	for _,word in ipairs(monsterWords) do
		if string.find(name,word,1,true) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end
local function isPlayerCharacter(model)
	for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if otherPlayer.Character == model then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end
local function isMonster(model)
	if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then
		return false
	end
	if isPlayerCharacter(model) then
		return false
	end
	if model:GetAttribute("IsMonster") == true
		or model:GetAttribute("Monster") == true
		or model:GetAttribute("IsEnemy") == true
		or model:GetAttribute("Enemy") == true then
		return true
	end
	if CollectionService:HasTag(model,"Monster")
		or CollectionService:HasTag(model,"Enemy")
		or CollectionService:HasTag(model,"Locust") then
		return true
	end
	return containsMonsterWord(model.Name)
end
local function findMonsterModel(object)
	local current = object
	while current and current ~= workspace do
		if current:IsA("Model") and isMonster(current) then
			return current
		end
		current = current.Parent
	end
	return nil
end
local function refreshMonsters()
	clearMarkerTable(monsterMarkers)
	if not monsterLocator then
		return
	end
	local found = {}
	local count = 0
	for _,object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("Model") then
			local monster = findMonsterModel(object)
			if monster
				and not found[monster]
				and not isPlayerCharacter(monster) then
				local monsterRoot = getRoot(monster)
				if monsterRoot
					and (
						not root
						or (root.Position-monsterRoot.Position).Magnitude <= scanRadius
					) then
					found[monster] = true
					createMarker(monster,monster.Name,true)
					count += 1
				end
			end
		end
	end
	setStatus("MONSTERS FOUND: "..tostring(count))
end
local function setMonsterLocator(value)
	monsterLocator = value
	setToggle(monsterButton,monsterLocator,"MONSTER LOCATOR")
	setToggle(quickLocust,monsterLocator,"LOCUST LOCATOR")
	if monsterLocator then
		refreshMonsters()
	else
		clearMarkerTable(monsterMarkers)
	end
	setStatus(monsterLocator and "MONSTER LOCATOR ON" or "MONSTER LOCATOR OFF")
end
monsterButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setMonsterLocator(not monsterLocator)
end)
refreshMonstersButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	if monsterLocator then
		refreshMonsters()
	else
		setStatus("MONSTER LOCATOR OFF")
	end
end)
clearMonsterButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	clearMarkerTable(monsterMarkers)
	setStatus("MONSTER MARKERS CLEARED")
end)
workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if not monsterLocator then
		return
	end
	task.defer(function()
		task.wait(.1)
		if not monsterLocator then
			return
		end
		local monster = findMonsterModel(object)
		if monster
			and not monsterMarkers[monster]
			and not isPlayerCharacter(monster) then
			createMarker(monster,monster.Name,true)
		end
	end)
end)
local markerTimer = 0
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	markerTimer += delta
	if markerTimer < .2 then
		return
	end
	markerTimer = 0
	if not root then
		return
	end
	for target,data in pairs(playerMarkers) do
		local targetRoot = getRoot(target)
		if not target.Parent or not targetRoot then
			removeMarker(playerMarkers,target)
		else
			local distance =
				math.floor((root.Position-targetRoot.Position).Magnitude)
			if distance > scanRadius then
				data.gui.Enabled = false
			else
				data.gui.Enabled = true
				data.distance.Text = "DISTANCE: "..tostring(distance)
			end
		end
	end
	for target,data in pairs(monsterMarkers) do
		local targetRoot = getRoot(target)
		if not target.Parent or not targetRoot then
			removeMarker(monsterMarkers,target)
		else
			local distance =
				math.floor((root.Position-targetRoot.Position).Magnitude)
			if distance > scanRadius then
				data.gui.Enabled = false
			else
				data.gui.Enabled = true
				data.distance.Text = "DISTANCE: "..tostring(distance)
			end
		end
	end
end)
clearMarkersButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setPlayerLocator(false)
	setMonsterLocator(false)
	clearMarkerTable(playerMarkers)
	clearMarkerTable(monsterMarkers)
	setStatus("ALL MARKERS CLEARED")
end)
quickFlight.Activated:Connect(function()
	setFlight(not flying)
end)
quickNoclip.Activated:Connect(function()
	setNoclip(not noclip)
end)
quickPlayers.Activated:Connect(function()
	setPlayerLocator(not playerLocator)
end)
quickLocust.Activated:Connect(function()
	setMonsterLocator(not monsterLocator)
end)
local openButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
openButton.Name = "NotRubyDragonButton"
openButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50,50)
openButton.Position = UDim2.new(1,-64,1,-66)
openButton.Text = "🐉"
openButton.TextSize = 25
openButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
openButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
openButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24,13,22)
openButton.Visible = false
openButton.AutoButtonColor = false
openButton.Active = true
openButton.Parent = gui
corner(openButton,1000)

do
	local miniGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	miniGradient.Name = "DragonGradient"
	miniGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromRGB(58,22,39)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(.52,Color3.fromRGB(23,16,29)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromRGB(8,10,17))
	})
	miniGradient.Rotation = 135
	miniGradient.Parent = openButton
end
;(function()
	local miniAspect = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
	miniAspect.Name = "MiniCircleAspect"
	miniAspect.AspectRatio = 1
	miniAspect.DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Width
	miniAspect.Parent = openButton
end)()
Instance.new("UIScale",openButton).Name = "SmoothScale"

-- Bottom-right dragon tab: resizable, movable and touch-safe.
addStroke(openButton,gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,2.4,.02)
openButton:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Name = "MiniOutline"
openButton:FindFirstChild("MiniOutline").ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
openButton:FindFirstChild("MiniOutline").Color = gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent
openButton:FindFirstChild("MiniOutline").Thickness = 2.4
openButton:FindFirstChild("MiniOutline").Transparency = .02
local miniBlockClickUntil = 0

do
local function setupMiniTabDrag()
	local miniDragging = false
	local miniDragInput
	local miniDragStart
	local miniStartAbsolute
	local miniStartUDimPosition
	local miniWasDragged = false

	openButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			return
		end

		-- Do not start dragging from the resize grip.
		local grip = openButton:FindFirstChild("MiniResizeGrip")
		if grip then
			local p = input.Position
			local gp = grip.AbsolutePosition
			local gs = grip.AbsoluteSize
			if p.X >= gp.X and p.X <= gp.X + gs.X
				and p.Y >= gp.Y and p.Y <= gp.Y + gs.Y then
				return
			end
		end

		miniDragging = true
		miniDragInput = input
		miniDragStart = input.Position
		miniStartAbsolute = openButton.AbsolutePosition
		miniStartUDimPosition = openButton.Position
		miniWasDragged = false
	end)

	UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not miniDragging
			or not miniDragStart
			or not miniStartAbsolute
			or not miniStartUDimPosition then
			return
		end

		local validInput =
			input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or (
				input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				and input == miniDragInput
			)

		if not validInput then
			return
		end

		local delta = input.Position - miniDragStart

		-- Ignore tiny finger jitter. Once a drag starts here it stays a
		-- move gesture until release, so it cannot suddenly become resize.
		if delta.Magnitude < 5 then
			return
		end

		if delta.Magnitude > 6 then
			miniWasDragged = true
		end

		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport =
			camera and camera.ViewportSize
			or Vector2.new(1000,700)

		local size = openButton.AbsoluteSize

		-- Preserve the original UDim2 and only apply the movement delta.
		-- This fixes the mobile jump-up bug caused by rebuilding Position
		-- from AbsolutePosition.
		local minDX = 4 - miniStartAbsolute.X
		local maxDX =
			viewport.X - miniStartAbsolute.X - size.X - 4
		local minDY = 4 - miniStartAbsolute.Y
		local maxDY =
			viewport.Y - miniStartAbsolute.Y - size.Y - 4

		local appliedX = math.clamp(
			delta.X,
			minDX,
			math.max(minDX,maxDX)
		)
		local appliedY = math.clamp(
			delta.Y,
			minDY,
			math.max(minDY,maxDY)
		)

		openButton.Position = UDim2.new(
			miniStartUDimPosition.X.Scale,
			miniStartUDimPosition.X.Offset + appliedX,
			miniStartUDimPosition.Y.Scale,
			miniStartUDimPosition.Y.Offset + appliedY
		)
	end)

	UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if not miniDragging then
			return
		end

		if input == miniDragInput
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then

			miniDragging = false
			miniDragInput = nil
			miniDragStart = nil
			miniStartAbsolute = nil
			miniStartUDimPosition = nil

			if miniWasDragged then
				miniBlockClickUntil = os.clock() + .20
			end
		end
	end)
end
safeSetup("Dragon Button Drag",setupMiniTabDrag)

end
do
local function setupMiniTabResize()
	local grip = Instance.new("TextButton")
	grip.Name = "MiniResizeGrip"
	grip.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,1)
	grip.Position = UDim2.new(1,-2,1,-2)
	grip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10,10)
	grip.Text = ""
	grip.TextSize = 1
	grip.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	grip.TextColor3 = accent
	grip.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38,16,22)
	grip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	grip.BorderSizePixel = 0
	grip.AutoButtonColor = false
	grip.Active = true
	grip.ZIndex = 220
	grip.Parent = openButton
	corner(grip,1000)

	local resizing = false
	local resizeCommitted = false
	local resizeInput
	local resizeStart
	local startSize
	local resizeStartPosition
	local resizeStartAbsolute

	grip.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			return
		end

		resizing = true
		resizeCommitted = false
		resizeInput = input
		resizeStart = input.Position
		startSize = openButton.AbsoluteSize
		resizeStartPosition = openButton.Position
		resizeStartAbsolute = openButton.AbsolutePosition
	end)

	UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not resizing
			or not resizeStart
			or not startSize
			or not resizeStartAbsolute then
			return
		end

		local valid =
			input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or (
				input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				and input == resizeInput
			)

		if not valid then
			return
		end

		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1000,700)
		local pos = resizeStartAbsolute
		local delta = input.Position - resizeStart

		-- Resize only after a deliberate movement from the tiny corner grip.
		-- This greatly reduces accidental resizing while moving the small tab.
		if not resizeCommitted then
			if delta.Magnitude < 7 then
				return
			end
			resizeCommitted = true
		end

		local startDiameter =
			math.max(startSize.X,startSize.Y)
		local startCenter =
			Vector2.new(
				pos.X + startSize.X*.5,
				pos.Y + startSize.Y*.5
			)

		-- Limit diameter equally around the center. This makes resizing look
		-- like one circle smoothly growing into another circle.
		local maxSize = math.max(
			34,
			math.min(
				140,
				(startCenter.X-4)*2,
				(viewport.X-startCenter.X-4)*2,
				(startCenter.Y-4)*2,
				(viewport.Y-startCenter.Y-4)*2
			)
		)

		local radialDelta =
			(delta.X+delta.Y)*.5

		local size = math.clamp(
			startDiameter + radialDelta,
			34,
			maxSize
		)

		local halfChange = (size-startDiameter)*.5

		openButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size,size)

		if resizeStartPosition then
			openButton.Position =
				UDim2.new(
					resizeStartPosition.X.Scale,
					resizeStartPosition.X.Offset-halfChange,
					resizeStartPosition.Y.Scale,
					resizeStartPosition.Y.Offset-halfChange
				)
		end

		local circleCorner = openButton:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
		if circleCorner then
			circleCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(.5,0)
		end

		-- Keep the mini photo tab proportional.
		openButton.TextSize = math.clamp(
			math.floor(size * .46),
			16,
			42
		)

		-- Keep the resize grip usable at all sizes.
		local gripSize = math.clamp(
			math.floor(size * .16),
			8,
			14
		)
		grip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(gripSize,gripSize)
		grip.TextSize = math.clamp(
			math.floor(gripSize * .55),
			7,
			12
		)
	end)

	UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if resizing
			and (
				input == resizeInput
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			) then
			resizing = false
			resizeCommitted = false
			resizeInput = nil
			resizeStart = nil
			startSize = nil
			resizeStartPosition = nil
			resizeStartAbsolute = nil
		end
	end)
end
safeSetup("Dragon Button Resize",setupMiniTabResize)

end
local function minimizePanel()
	if menuDeleted
		or not panel.Visible
		or panel:GetAttribute("MenuTransitionBusy") then
		return
	end

	panel:SetAttribute("MenuTransitionBusy",true)

	-- Remember the user's exact panel layout so the animation can travel
	-- to the mini tab without changing their saved size/position.
	panel:SetAttribute("MenuRestoreXS",panel.Position.X.Scale)
	panel:SetAttribute("MenuRestoreXO",panel.Position.X.Offset)
	panel:SetAttribute("MenuRestoreYS",panel.Position.Y.Scale)
	panel:SetAttribute("MenuRestoreYO",panel.Position.Y.Offset)
	panel:SetAttribute("MenuRestoreW",panel.Size.X.Offset)
	panel:SetAttribute("MenuRestoreH",panel.Size.Y.Offset)

	local restorePosition = panel.Position
	local restoreSize = Vector2.new(
		panel.Size.X.Offset,
		panel.Size.Y.Offset
	)
	local oldClips = panel.ClipsDescendants
	local panelScale = panel:FindFirstChild("SmoothScale")
	local restorePanelScale =
		tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale")) or 1

	-- Exact mini-tab destination in screen pixels.
	local miniPosition = openButton.AbsolutePosition
	local miniSize = openButton.AbsoluteSize
	local miniCenter =
		Vector2.new(
			miniPosition.X + miniSize.X*.5,
			miniPosition.Y + miniSize.Y*.5
		)

	local panelPosition = panel.AbsolutePosition
	local panelCenter =
		Vector2.new(
			panelPosition.X + restoreSize.X*.5,
			panelPosition.Y + restoreSize.Y*.5
		)

	-- PHASE 1: the body disappears and the remaining header/core compresses
	-- into a sleek bar while beginning to move toward the mini tab.
	local coreWidth = math.clamp(restoreSize.X*.52,190,300)
	local coreHeight = 58
	local coreCenter = panelCenter:Lerp(miniCenter,.46)
	local corePosition =
		UDim2.fromOffset(
			coreCenter.X-coreWidth*.5,
			coreCenter.Y-coreHeight*.5
		)

	panel.ClipsDescendants = true
	if panelScale then
		panelScale.Scale = 1
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local phaseOne =
			TweenService:Create(
				panel,
				TweenInfo.new(
					.19,
					Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
					Enum.EasingDirection.InOut
				),
				{
					Position = corePosition,
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(coreWidth,coreHeight)
				}
			)

		phaseOne:Play()

		-- Let the full menu start the movement, then collapse to just its
		-- top/core part. This gives the "one part, then the other" effect.
		task.delay(.08,function()
			if panel:GetAttribute("MenuTransitionBusy") then
				sidebar.Visible = false
				content.Visible = false
			end
		end)

		phaseOne.Completed:Wait()
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		-- PHASE 2: the compressed core gets pulled all the way into the
		-- circular mini tab.
		local phaseTwo =
			TweenService:Create(
				panel,
				TweenInfo.new(
					.16,
					Enum.EasingStyle.Quart,
					Enum.EasingDirection.In
				),
				{
					Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
						miniPosition.X,
						miniPosition.Y
					),
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
						math.max(34,miniSize.X),
						math.max(34,miniSize.Y)
					)
				}
			)

		phaseTwo:Play()
		phaseTwo.Completed:Wait()
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		panel.Visible = false

		-- Restore the real panel layout while hidden so reopening never
		-- damages the user's custom size or location.
		panel.Position = restorePosition
		panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
			restoreSize.X,
			restoreSize.Y
		)
		panel.ClipsDescendants = oldClips
		sidebar.Visible = true
		content.Visible = true
		if panelScale then
			panelScale.Scale = restorePanelScale
		end

		local miniScale = openButton:FindFirstChild("SmoothScale")
		if miniScale then
			miniScale.Scale = .48
		end

		openButton.Visible = true

		if miniScale then
			TweenService:Create(
				miniScale,
				TweenInfo.new(
					.24,
					Enum.EasingStyle.Back,
					Enum.EasingDirection.Out
				),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end

		panel:SetAttribute("MenuTransitionBusy",false)
	end)

	flightControls.Visible = flying
end

local function reopenPanel()
	if menuDeleted
		or panel:GetAttribute("MenuTransitionBusy") then
		return
	end

	panel:SetAttribute("MenuTransitionBusy",true)
	flightControls.Visible = flying

	-- Rebuild the exact panel layout saved when it minimized.
	local restorePosition =
		UDim2.new(
			panel:GetAttribute("MenuRestoreXS")
				or panel.Position.X.Scale,
			panel:GetAttribute("MenuRestoreXO")
				or panel.Position.X.Offset,
			panel:GetAttribute("MenuRestoreYS")
				or panel.Position.Y.Scale,
			panel:GetAttribute("MenuRestoreYO")
				or panel.Position.Y.Offset
		)

	local restoreWidth =
		panel:GetAttribute("MenuRestoreW")
		or panel.Size.X.Offset
	local restoreHeight =
		panel:GetAttribute("MenuRestoreH")
		or panel.Size.Y.Offset

	local miniPosition = openButton.AbsolutePosition
	local miniSize = openButton.AbsoluteSize
	local panelScale = panel:FindFirstChild("SmoothScale")
	local miniScale = openButton:FindFirstChild("SmoothScale")
	local restorePanelScale =
		tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale")) or 1
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport =
		camera
		and camera.ViewportSize
		or Vector2.new(800,600)

	-- Start as a tiny core exactly where the circular mini tab sits.
	panel.Position =
		UDim2.fromOffset(miniPosition.X,miniPosition.Y)
	panel.Size =
		UDim2.fromOffset(
			math.max(34,miniSize.X),
			math.max(34,miniSize.Y)
		)
	panel.ClipsDescendants = true
	sidebar.Visible = false
	content.Visible = false
	panel.Visible = true
	if panelScale then
		panelScale.Scale = 1
	end

	-- PHASE 1: mini tab grows into the menu's first/core part at center.
	local coreWidth = math.clamp(restoreWidth*.52,190,300)
	local coreHeight = 58
	local centerCorePosition =
		UDim2.fromOffset(
			viewport.X*.5-coreWidth*.5,
			viewport.Y*.5-coreHeight*.5
		)

	task.spawn(function()
		if miniScale then
			TweenService:Create(
				miniScale,
				TweenInfo.new(
					.12,
					Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
					Enum.EasingDirection.In
				),
				{Scale = .35}
			):Play()
		end

		local phaseOne =
			TweenService:Create(
				panel,
				TweenInfo.new(
					.22,
					Enum.EasingStyle.Back,
					Enum.EasingDirection.Out
				),
				{
					Position = centerCorePosition,
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(coreWidth,coreHeight)
				}
			)

		phaseOne:Play()
		task.delay(.09,function()
			if not menuDeleted then
				openButton.Visible = false
			end
		end)
		phaseOne.Completed:Wait()
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		-- PHASE 2: the rest of the menu unfolds from that center core into
		-- the user's real panel size/location.
		sidebar.Visible = true
		content.Visible = true

		local phaseTwo =
			TweenService:Create(
				panel,
				TweenInfo.new(
					.25,
					Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
					Enum.EasingDirection.Out
				),
				{
					Position = restorePosition,
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
						restoreWidth,
						restoreHeight
					)
				}
			)

		phaseTwo:Play()
		phaseTwo.Completed:Wait()
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		panel.ClipsDescendants = false
		setPanelScale(restorePanelScale,false)
		if miniScale then
			miniScale.Scale = 1
		end
		clampGuiObjectToViewport(panel,4)
		setTab(currentTab)
		panel:SetAttribute("MenuTransitionBusy",false)
	end)
end
do
local function setupDeleteConfirmation()
	local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
	overlay.Name = "DestroyConfirmation"
	overlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
	overlay.BackgroundTransparency = .28
	overlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
	overlay.Visible = false
	overlay.Active = true
	overlay.ZIndex = 900
	overlay.Parent = gui

	local card = Instance.new("Frame")
	card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
	card.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
	card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(380,205)
	card.BackgroundColor3 = BG
	card.BorderSizePixel = 0
	card.ZIndex = 901
	card.Parent = overlay
	corner(card,18)
	addStroke(card,Color3.fromRGB(235,68,88),1.7,.02)

	local warning = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	warning.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	warning.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,18)
	warning.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,28)
	warning.Text = "DESTROY NOT RUBY V1?"
	warning.TextSize = 17
	warning.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	warning.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,92,108)
	warning.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	warning.ZIndex = 902
	warning.Parent = card

	local message = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	message.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	message.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20,52)
	message.Size = UDim2.new(1,-40,0,70)
	message.Text = "Are you sure you want to destroy the menu?\nIt will disappear completely with no mini tab."
	message.TextWrapped = true
	message.TextSize = 11
	message.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	message.TextColor3 = TEXT
	message.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	message.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	message.ZIndex = 902
	message.Parent = card

	local noButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	noButton.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-24,0,44)
	noButton.Position = UDim2.new(0,16,1,-60)
	noButton.Text = "NO"
	noButton.TextSize = 11
	noButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	noButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
	noButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	noButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	noButton.ZIndex = 902
	noButton.Parent = card
	corner(noButton,11)

	local yesButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	yesButton.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-24,0,44)
	yesButton.Position = UDim2.new(.5,8,1,-60)
	yesButton.Text = "YES, DESTROY"
	yesButton.TextSize = 10
	yesButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	yesButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
	yesButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(166,42,58)
	yesButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	yesButton.ZIndex = 902
	yesButton.Parent = card
	corner(yesButton,11)

	local stage = 1

	local function closeVisibleMenuKeepFeatures()
		-- Keep the ScreenGui alive for feature visuals such as markers/HUD,
		-- but remove every control that could reopen or control the menu.
		if flightControls and flightControls.Parent then
			flightControls.Visible = false
		end
		if openButton and openButton.Parent then
			openButton:Destroy()
		end
		if panel and panel.Parent then
			panel:Destroy()
		end
		if overlay and overlay.Parent then
			overlay:Destroy()
		end
	end

	local function destroyAndDisableEverything()
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end
		menuDeleted = true
		universalCleanup()
		remakeLabsCleanup()
		setFlight(false)
		setSprint(false)
		setNoclip(false)
		resetVisuals()
		setPlayerLocator(false)
		setMonsterLocator(false)
		clearMarkerTable(playerMarkers)
		clearMarkerTable(monsterMarkers)
		if openButton and openButton.Parent then
			openButton.Visible = false
		end
		if flightControls and flightControls.Parent then
			flightControls.Visible = false
		end
		if gui and gui.Parent then
			gui:Destroy()
		end
	end

	closeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end
		stage = 1
		warning.Text = "DESTROY NOT RUBY V1?"
		message.Text = "Are you sure you want to destroy the menu?\nIt will disappear completely with no mini tab."
		noButton.Text = "NO"
		yesButton.Text = "YES, DESTROY"
		overlay.Visible = true
	end)

	noButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if stage == 1 then
			overlay.Visible = false
			return
		end

		-- NO on the second question means keep every currently active
		-- feature running. There will be no menu left to turn them off.
		closeVisibleMenuKeepFeatures()
	end)

	yesButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		if stage == 1 then
			stage = 2
			warning.Text = "DISABLE ACTIVE FEATURES?"
			message.Text =
				"The menu will be destroyed.\n"
				.."Do you also want to disable all active features?\n\n"
				.."NO = active features stay on until you leave the game."
			noButton.Text = "NO, KEEP ON"
			yesButton.Text = "YES, DISABLE"
			return
		end

		destroyAndDisableEverything()
	end)
end
safeSetup("Delete Confirmation",setupDeleteConfirmation)
end
minimizeButton.Activated:Connect(minimizePanel)
minimizeSettingsButton.Activated:Connect(minimizePanel)
openButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	if os.clock() < miniBlockClickUntil then
		return
	end
	reopenPanel()
end)
do
local function setupResizing()
local MIN_PANEL_WIDTH = 340
local MIN_PANEL_HEIGHT = 300
local EDGE_SIZE = 12
local CORNER_SIZE = 24
local SCREEN_MARGIN = 4
local resizing = false
local resizeInput
local resizeDirection
local resizeStart
local resizeStartPosition
local resizeStartUDimPosition
local resizeStartSize
local resizeZones = {}
local function beginResize(direction,input)
	if resizePinned then
		return
	end
	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
		and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		return
	end
	resizing = true
	resizeInput = input
	resizeDirection = direction
	resizeStart = input.Position
	resizeStartPosition = panel.AbsolutePosition
	resizeStartUDimPosition = panel.Position
	resizeStartSize = Vector2.new(
		panel.Size.X.Offset,
		panel.Size.Y.Offset
	)
	input.Changed:Connect(function()
		if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
			resizing = false
			resizeInput = nil
			resizeDirection = nil
		end
	end)
end
local function createResizeZone(name,direction,size,position,anchorPoint)
	local zone = Instance.new("TextButton")
	zone.Name = name
	zone.Size = size
	zone.Position = position
	zone.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint or Vector2.zero
	zone.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	zone.BorderSizePixel = 0
	zone.Text = ""
	zone.AutoButtonColor = false
	zone.Active = true
	zone.ZIndex = 120
	zone.Parent = panel
	zone.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		beginResize(direction,input)
	end)
	table.insert(resizeZones,zone)
	return zone
end
createResizeZone(
	"ResizeTop",
	"top",
	UDim2.new(1,-CORNER_SIZE * 2,0,EDGE_SIZE),
	UDim2.fromOffset(CORNER_SIZE,0)
)
createResizeZone(
	"ResizeBottom",
	"bottom",
	UDim2.new(1,-CORNER_SIZE * 2,0,EDGE_SIZE),
	UDim2.new(0,CORNER_SIZE,1,0),
	Vector2.new(0,1)
)
createResizeZone(
	"ResizeLeft",
	"left",
	UDim2.new(0,EDGE_SIZE,1,-CORNER_SIZE * 2),
	UDim2.fromOffset(0,CORNER_SIZE)
)
createResizeZone(
	"ResizeRight",
	"right",
	UDim2.new(0,EDGE_SIZE,1,-CORNER_SIZE * 2),
	UDim2.new(1,0,0,CORNER_SIZE),
	Vector2.new(1,0)
)
createResizeZone(
	"ResizeTopLeft",
	"topLeft",
	UDim2.fromOffset(CORNER_SIZE,CORNER_SIZE),
	UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
)
createResizeZone(
	"ResizeTopRight",
	"topRight",
	UDim2.fromOffset(CORNER_SIZE,CORNER_SIZE),
	UDim2.new(1,0,0,0),
	Vector2.new(1,0)
)
createResizeZone(
	"ResizeBottomLeft",
	"bottomLeft",
	UDim2.fromOffset(CORNER_SIZE,CORNER_SIZE),
	UDim2.new(0,0,1,0),
	Vector2.new(0,1)
)
createResizeZone(
	"ResizeBottomRight",
	"bottomRight",
	UDim2.fromOffset(CORNER_SIZE,CORNER_SIZE),
	UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
	Vector2.new(1,1)
)
local function updateResize(inputPosition)
	if resizePinned
		or not resizing
		or not resizeDirection
		or not resizeStart
		or not resizeStartPosition
		or not resizeStartUDimPosition
		or not resizeStartSize then
		return
	end
	local delta = inputPosition - resizeStart
	camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport =
		camera and camera.ViewportSize
		or Vector2.new(1000,700)
	local startLeft = resizeStartPosition.X
	local startTop = resizeStartPosition.Y
	local startWidth = resizeStartSize.X
	local startHeight = resizeStartSize.Y
	local panelScaleValue =
		math.max(.01,tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale")) or 1)
	local fromLeft =
		resizeDirection == "left"
		or resizeDirection == "topLeft"
		or resizeDirection == "bottomLeft"
	local fromRight =
		resizeDirection == "right"
		or resizeDirection == "topRight"
		or resizeDirection == "bottomRight"
	local fromTop =
		resizeDirection == "top"
		or resizeDirection == "topLeft"
		or resizeDirection == "topRight"
	local fromBottom =
		resizeDirection == "bottom"
		or resizeDirection == "bottomLeft"
		or resizeDirection == "bottomRight"
	local appliedX = 0
	local appliedY = 0
	local newWidth = startWidth
	local newHeight = startHeight
	if fromLeft then
		local maxLeftDelta =
			(startWidth - MIN_PANEL_WIDTH)*panelScaleValue
		local minLeftDelta = math.min(
			SCREEN_MARGIN - startLeft,
			maxLeftDelta
		)
		appliedX = math.clamp(
			delta.X,
			minLeftDelta,
			maxLeftDelta
		)
		newWidth = startWidth - appliedX/panelScaleValue
	elseif fromRight then
		local availableWidth = math.max(
			MIN_PANEL_WIDTH,
			(viewport.X - startLeft - SCREEN_MARGIN)
			/panelScaleValue
		)
		newWidth = math.clamp(
			startWidth + delta.X/panelScaleValue,
			MIN_PANEL_WIDTH,
			availableWidth
		)
	end
	if fromTop then
		local maxTopDelta =
			(startHeight - MIN_PANEL_HEIGHT)*panelScaleValue
		local minTopDelta = math.min(
			SCREEN_MARGIN - startTop,
			maxTopDelta
		)
		appliedY = math.clamp(
			delta.Y,
			minTopDelta,
			maxTopDelta
		)
		newHeight = startHeight - appliedY/panelScaleValue
	elseif fromBottom then
		local availableHeight = math.max(
			MIN_PANEL_HEIGHT,
			(viewport.Y - startTop - SCREEN_MARGIN)
			/panelScaleValue
		)
		newHeight = math.clamp(
			startHeight + delta.Y/panelScaleValue,
			MIN_PANEL_HEIGHT,
			availableHeight
		)
	end
	panel.Position = UDim2.new(
		resizeStartUDimPosition.X.Scale,
		resizeStartUDimPosition.X.Offset + appliedX,
		resizeStartUDimPosition.Y.Scale,
		resizeStartUDimPosition.Y.Offset + appliedY
	)
	panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(newWidth,newHeight)
end
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if not resizing then
		return
	end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
		updateResize(input.Position)
	elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
		and input == resizeInput then
		updateResize(input.Position)
	end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if input == resizeInput
		or (
			resizing
			and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
		) then
		resizing = false
		resizeInput = nil
		resizeDirection = nil
	end
end)
end
safeSetup("Panel Resize",setupResizing)
end
do
local function setupDragging()
local dragging = false
local dragInput
local dragStart
local dragStartPosition
local dragStartAbsolute
header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if panelPinned then
		return
	end
	local position = input.Position
	local closePos = closeButton.AbsolutePosition
	local closeSize = closeButton.AbsoluteSize
	local minPos = minimizeButton.AbsolutePosition
	local minSize = minimizeButton.AbsoluteSize
	local insideClose =
		position.X >= closePos.X
		and position.X <= closePos.X + closeSize.X
		and position.Y >= closePos.Y
		and position.Y <= closePos.Y + closeSize.Y
	local insideMin =
		position.X >= minPos.X
		and position.X <= minPos.X + minSize.X
		and position.Y >= minPos.Y
		and position.Y <= minPos.Y + minSize.Y
	if insideClose or insideMin then
		return
	end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
		or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		dragging = true
		dragInput = input
		dragStart = input.Position
		dragStartPosition = panel.Position
		dragStartAbsolute = panel.AbsolutePosition
	end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if panelPinned or not dragging then
		return
	end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
		or (
			input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			and input == dragInput
		) then
		local delta = input.Position - dragStart
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport =
			camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1000,700)
		local size = panel.AbsoluteSize
		local startAbsolute = dragStartAbsolute or panel.AbsolutePosition
		local minX = 4-startAbsolute.X
		local maxX = viewport.X-startAbsolute.X-size.X-4
		local minY = 4-startAbsolute.Y
		local maxY = viewport.Y-startAbsolute.Y-size.Y-4
		local appliedX =
			math.clamp(delta.X,minX,math.max(minX,maxX))
		local appliedY =
			math.clamp(delta.Y,minY,math.max(minY,maxY))
		panel.Position = UDim2.new(
			dragStartPosition.X.Scale,
			dragStartPosition.X.Offset + appliedX,
			dragStartPosition.Y.Scale,
			dragStartPosition.Y.Offset + appliedY
		)
	end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if input == dragInput
		or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		dragging = false
		dragInput = nil
		dragStartAbsolute = nil
	end
end)
end
safeSetup("Panel Drag",setupDragging)
end

	setToggle(pinSizeButton,resizePinned,"PIN SIZE")
	setStatus(resizePinned and "SIZE PINNED" or "SIZE UNPINNED")

local compactRestoreSize
local compactRestorePosition
compactButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	compactMode = not compactMode
	setToggle(compactButton,compactMode,"COMPACT MODE")
	if compactMode then
		compactRestoreSize = Vector2.new(
			panel.Size.X.Offset,
			panel.Size.Y.Offset
		)
		compactRestorePosition = panel.Position
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport =
			camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
		local compactWidth =
			math.max(340,math.min(440,viewport.X-18))
		local compactHeight =
			math.max(300,math.min(330,viewport.Y-30))
		panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(compactWidth,compactHeight)
		panel.Position = UDim2.new(
			.5,
			-compactWidth*.5,
			.5,
			-compactHeight*.5
		)
	else
		if compactRestoreSize then
			panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
				compactRestoreSize.X,
				compactRestoreSize.Y
			)
		else
			panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(620,460)
		end
		if compactRestorePosition then
			panel.Position = compactRestorePosition
		end
		task.defer(function()
			clampGuiObjectToViewport(panel,4)
		end)
	end
	setPanelScale(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale"),false)
	setStatus(compactMode and "COMPACT MODE ON" or "COMPACT MODE OFF")
end)
centerButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	panel.Position = UDim2.new(
		.5,
		-math.floor(panel.AbsoluteSize.X/2),
		.5,
		-math.floor(panel.AbsoluteSize.Y/2)
	)
	setStatus("PANEL CENTERED")
end)

	cycleTheme()
	themeButton.Text = "THEME: "..themes[themeIndex].name

	-- Non-outline accent elements.
	for _,button in ipairs({upButton,downButton}) do
		button.TextColor3 =
			button.BackgroundColor3 == accent
			and ACTIVE_TEXT
			or TEXT
	end
end)

outlineButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	local choices = {
		{name = "MATCH", color = nil},
		{name = "RUBY", color = Color3.fromRGB(255,42,64)},
		{name = "BLUE", color = Color3.fromRGB(65,155,255)},
		{name = "PURPLE", color = Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255)},
		{name = "GREEN", color = Color3.fromRGB(55,225,135)},
		{name = "GOLD", color = Color3.fromRGB(255,195,60)},
		{name = "CYAN", color = Color3.fromRGB(60,225,240)},
		{name = "WHITE", color = Color3.fromRGB(245,245,250)}
	}

	local index = tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("OutlineIndex")) or 1
	index += 1
	if index > #choices then
		index = 1
	end

	local choice = choices[index]
	local chosenColor = choice.color or accent

	gui:SetAttribute("OutlineIndex",index)
	gui:SetAttribute(
		"OutlineMode",
		choice.color == nil and "MATCH" or "CUSTOM"
	)
	gui:SetAttribute("OutlineColor",chosenColor)

	for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("UIStroke")
			and object:GetAttribute("NotRubyFixedColor") ~= true then
			object.Color = chosenColor
		end
	end

	outlineButton.Text = "OUTLINE: "..choice.name
	setStatus("OUTLINE: "..choice.name)
end)
resetLayoutButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	compactMode = false
	setToggle(compactButton,false,"COMPACT MODE")
	camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport =
		camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
	local width = math.max(340,math.min(620,viewport.X-24))
	local height = math.max(300,math.min(460,viewport.Y-44))
	panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width,height)
	panel.Position = UDim2.new(.5,-width*.5,.5,-height*.5)
	setPanelScale(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale"),false)
	setStatus("LAYOUT RESET")
end)

	panelPinned = false
	setToggle(pinSizeButton,false,"PIN SIZE")
	setToggle(pinPanelButton,false,"PIN PANEL")
	setStatus("PANEL PINS RELEASED")
end)

	setFlight(false)
	setSprint(false)
	setNoclip(false)

	sprintBox.Text = "42"
	flyBox.Text = "70"
	radiusBox.Text = "5000"
	applyMovementStats()
	setStatus("ALL FEATURES RESET")
end
