	local hudResizeGrip = Instance.new("TextButton")
	hudResizeGrip.Name = "ResizeGrip"
	hudResizeGrip.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,1)
	hudResizeGrip.Position = UDim2.new(1,-5,1,-5)
	hudResizeGrip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28,28)
	hudResizeGrip.Text = "↘"
	hudResizeGrip.TextSize = 14
	hudResizeGrip.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	hudResizeGrip.TextColor3 = accent
	hudResizeGrip.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	hudResizeGrip.BorderSizePixel = 0
	hudResizeGrip.AutoButtonColor = false
	hudResizeGrip.Active = true
	hudResizeGrip.ZIndex = 405
	hudResizeGrip.Parent = hud
	corner(hudResizeGrip,9)
	addStroke(hudResizeGrip,accent,1,.18)

	local hudText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	hudText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	hudText.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11,34)
	hudText.Size = UDim2.new(1,-48,1,-42)
	hudText.Text = "FPS: --   SPEED: --\nPOSITION: --\nHEADING: --   GRAVITY: --"
	hudText.TextSize = 10
	hudText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	hudText.TextColor3 = TEXT
	hudText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	hudText.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	hudText.TextWrapped = true
	hudText.ZIndex = 403
	hudText.Parent = hud

	local hudDragging = false
	local hudDragInput = nil
	local hudDragStart = nil
	local hudStartPosition = nil
	local hudStartAbsolute = nil

	hudDragBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			return
		end

		hudDragging = true
		hudDragInput = input
		hudDragStart = input.Position
		hudStartPosition = hud.Position
		hudStartAbsolute = hud.AbsolutePosition
	end)

	local hudResizing = false
	local hudResizeInput = nil
	local hudResizeStart = nil
	local hudStartSize = nil

	hudResizeGrip.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			return
		end

		hudResizing = true
		hudResizeInput = input
		hudResizeStart = input.Position
		hudStartSize = hud.AbsoluteSize
	end)

	UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if hudDragging
			and hudDragStart
			and hudStartPosition
			and hudStartAbsolute
			and (
				input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
				or (
					input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
					and input == hudDragInput
				)
			) then

			local delta = input.Position-hudDragStart
			camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local viewport =
				camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1000,700)
			local size = hud.AbsoluteSize
			local minX = 4-hudStartAbsolute.X
			local maxX =
				viewport.X-hudStartAbsolute.X-size.X-4
			local minY = 4-hudStartAbsolute.Y
			local maxY =
				viewport.Y-hudStartAbsolute.Y-size.Y-4
			local x = math.clamp(delta.X,minX,math.max(minX,maxX))
			local y = math.clamp(delta.Y,minY,math.max(minY,maxY))

			hud.Position = UDim2.new(
				hudStartPosition.X.Scale,
				hudStartPosition.X.Offset+x,
				hudStartPosition.Y.Scale,
				hudStartPosition.Y.Offset+y
			)
		end

		if hudResizing
			and hudResizeStart
			and hudStartSize
			and (
				input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
				or (
					input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
					and input == hudResizeInput
				)
			) then

			camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local viewport =
				camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1000,700)
			local delta = input.Position-hudResizeStart
			local position = hud.AbsolutePosition
			local maxWidth = math.max(
				230,
				math.min(560,viewport.X-position.X-4)
			)
			local maxHeight = math.max(
				110,
				math.min(320,viewport.Y-position.Y-4)
			)
			hud.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
				math.clamp(hudStartSize.X+delta.X,230,maxWidth),
				math.clamp(hudStartSize.Y+delta.Y,110,maxHeight)
			)
		end
	end)

	UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if hudDragging
			and (
				input == hudDragInput
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			) then
			hudDragging = false
			hudDragInput = nil
			hudDragStart = nil
			hudStartPosition = nil
			hudStartAbsolute = nil
		end

		if hudResizing
			and (
				input == hudResizeInput
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			) then
			hudResizing = false
			hudResizeInput = nil
			hudResizeStart = nil
			hudStartSize = nil
		end
	end)

	local fps = 0
	local frameCount = 0
	local frameTimer = 0

	local function cardinalFromLook(look)
		local degrees =
			(math.deg(math.atan2(-look.X,-look.Z))+360)%360

		if degrees < 22.5 or degrees >= 337.5 then
			return "N"
		elseif degrees < 67.5 then
			return "NE"
		elseif degrees < 112.5 then
			return "E"
		elseif degrees < 157.5 then
			return "SE"
		elseif degrees < 202.5 then
			return "S"
		elseif degrees < 247.5 then
			return "SW"
		elseif degrees < 292.5 then
			return "W"
		end

		return "NW"
	end

	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		frameCount += 1
		frameTimer += delta

		if frameTimer >= .5 then
			fps = math.floor(frameCount/frameTimer+.5)
			frameCount = 0
			frameTimer = 0
		end

		if not liveHudEnabled or not hud.Parent then
			return
		end

		local currentRoot =
			root and root.Parent
			and root
			or (
				player.Character
				and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			)
		local position =
			currentRoot and currentRoot.Position or Vector3.zero
		local speed =
			currentRoot
			and currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
			or 0
		local heading =
			currentRoot
			and cardinalFromLook(currentRoot.CFrame.LookVector)
			or "--"

		hudText.Text = string.format(
			"FPS: %d   SPEED: %d\nPOSITION: X %d   Y %d   Z %d\nHEADING: %s   GRAVITY: %d",
			fps,
			math.floor(speed+.5),
			math.floor(position.X+.5),
			math.floor(position.Y+.5),
			math.floor(position.Z+.5),
			heading,
			math.floor(workspace.Gravity+.5)
		)
	end)

	gravityApply.Activated:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(gravityBox.Text)
		if not value then
			setStatus("INVALID GRAVITY")
			return
		end

		workspace.Gravity = math.clamp(value,0,500)
		gravityBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(workspace.Gravity))
		setStatus("GRAVITY "..tostring(math.floor(workspace.Gravity)))
	end)

	resetGravityButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		workspace.Gravity = originalGravity
		gravityBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(originalGravity))
		setStatus("GRAVITY RESET")
	end)

	hudButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		liveHudEnabled = not liveHudEnabled
		local scale = hud:FindFirstChild("SmoothScale")

		if liveHudEnabled then
			hud.Visible = true
			if scale then
				scale.Scale = .88
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
					{Scale = 1}
				):Play()
			end
		else
			hud.Visible = false
		end

		setToggle(hudButton,liveHudEnabled,"LIVE HUD")
		setStatus(liveHudEnabled and "LIVE HUD ON" or "LIVE HUD OFF")
	end)

	freezeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not root or not root.Parent then
			loadCharacter(2)
		end

		if not root then
			setStatus("CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE")
			return
		end

		frozen = not frozen
		if frozen then
			frozenRoot = root
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			root.Anchored = true
		elseif frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
			frozenRoot = nil
		end

		setToggle(freezeButton,frozen,"FREEZE")
		setStatus(frozen and "CHARACTER FROZEN" or "CHARACTER UNFROZEN")
	end)

	sitButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
			loadCharacter(2)
		end

		if humanoid then
			humanoid.Sit = not humanoid.Sit
			setStatus(humanoid.Sit and "SITTING" or "STANDING")
		else
			setStatus("CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE")
		end
	end)

	jumpButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not loadCharacter(2) then
			setStatus("CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE")
			return
		end

		if frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
			frozenRoot = nil
			frozen = false
			setToggle(freezeButton,false,"FREEZE")
		end

		humanoid.Sit = false
		humanoid.Jump = true
		pcall(function()
			humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
		end)
		setStatus("JUMP")
	end)

	local function isPerformanceObject(object)
		return object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
			or object:IsA("Trail")
			or object:IsA("Beam")
			or object:IsA("Smoke")
			or object:IsA("Fire")
			or object:IsA("Sparkles")
	end

	local function disablePerformanceObject(object)
		if not performanceMode or not isPerformanceObject(object) then
			return
		end

		if performanceStates[object] == nil then
			performanceStates[object] = object.Enabled
		end
		object.Enabled = false
	end

	local function restorePerformance()
		for object,wasEnabled in pairs(performanceStates) do
			if object and object.Parent then
				object.Enabled = wasEnabled
			end
		end
		table.clear(performanceStates)
		Lighting.GlobalShadows = originalShadows
	end

	local function setPerformance(value)
		performanceMode = value == true
		if performanceMode then
			originalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
			Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
			for _,object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				disablePerformanceObject(object)
			end
		else
			restorePerformance()
		end

		setToggle(performanceButton,performanceMode,"PERFORMANCE")
		setStatus(
			performanceMode
			and "PERFORMANCE MODE ON"
			or "PERFORMANCE MODE OFF"
		)
	end

	workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
		if menuDeleted or not performanceMode then
			return
		end
		task.defer(function()
			if not menuDeleted then
				disablePerformanceObject(object)
			end
		end)
	end)

	performanceButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setPerformance(not performanceMode)
	end)

	cameraResetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		loadCharacter(1)

		if camera then
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			if humanoid then
				camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
			end
			camera.FieldOfView = originalFOV
			fovBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(originalFOV))
			setStatus("CAMERA RESET")
		else
			setStatus("CAMERA UNAVAILABLE")
		end
	end)

	local function restoreInvisible()
		for object,data in pairs(invisibleOriginal) do
			if object and object.Parent then
				if data.kind == "part" then
					object.LocalTransparencyModifier = data.value
				else
					object.Transparency = data.value
				end
			end
		end
		table.clear(invisibleOriginal)
	end

	local function applyInvisible()
		local currentCharacter = player.Character
		if not currentCharacter then
			return false
		end

		for _,object in ipairs(currentCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("BasePart") then
				if invisibleOriginal[object] == nil then
					invisibleOriginal[object] = {
						kind = "part",
						value = object.LocalTransparencyModifier
					}
				end
				object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
			elseif object:IsA("Decal") then
				if invisibleOriginal[object] == nil then
					invisibleOriginal[object] = {
						kind = "decal",
						value = object.Transparency
					}
				end
				object.Transparency = 1
			end
		end

		return true
	end

	local function setInvisible(value)
		value = value == true
		if invisibleEnabled == value then
			return
		end

		invisibleEnabled = value
		gui:SetAttribute("InvisibleEnabled",invisibleEnabled)

		if invisibleEnabled then
			if not applyInvisible() then
				invisibleEnabled = false
				gui:SetAttribute("InvisibleEnabled",false)
				setStatus("CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE")
			end
		else
			restoreInvisible()
		end

		setToggle(invisibleButton,invisibleEnabled,"INVISIBLE")
		setStatus(
			invisibleEnabled
			and "INVISIBLE LOCAL VIEW ON"
			or "INVISIBLE LOCAL VIEW OFF"
		)
	end

	invisibleButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setInvisible(not invisibleEnabled)
	end)

	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("CommandInvisible"):Connect(function()
		setInvisible(not invisibleEnabled)
	end)

	local function clearSelfHighlight()
		if selfHighlight and selfHighlight.Parent then
			selfHighlight:Destroy()
		end
		selfHighlight = nil
	end

	local function setSelfHighlight(value)
		selfHighlightEnabled = value == true
		clearSelfHighlight()

		if selfHighlightEnabled and player.Character then
			selfHighlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
			selfHighlight.Name = "NotRubySelfAura"
			selfHighlight.Adornee = player.Character
			selfHighlight.FillColor = accent
			selfHighlight.OutlineColor = TEXT
			selfHighlight.FillTransparency = .72
			selfHighlight.OutlineTransparency = .05
			selfHighlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			selfHighlight.Parent = player.Character
		end

		setToggle(auraButton,selfHighlightEnabled,"SELF AURA")
		setStatus(selfHighlightEnabled and "SELF AURA ON" or "SELF AURA OFF")
	end

	auraButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setSelfHighlight(not selfHighlightEnabled)
	end)

	sparkButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not loadCharacter(1) or not root then
			setStatus("CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE")
			return
		end

		local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
		attachment.Name = "NotRubySparkBurst"
		attachment.Parent = root

		local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
		emitter.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
		emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,accent),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,TEXT)
		})
		emitter.LightEmission = 1
		emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(.7,1.3)
		emitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(7,14)
		emitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180,180)
		emitter.Rate = 0
		emitter.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0,360)
		emitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-120,120)
		emitter.Parent = attachment
		emitter:Emit(48)

		task.delay(1.6,function()
			if attachment and attachment.Parent then
				attachment:Destroy()
			end
		end)
		setStatus("SPARK BURST")
	end)

	refreshCharacterButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setStatus("REFRESHING CHARACTER REFERENCES")
		if loadCharacter(3) then
			applyMovementStats()
			if playerLocator then
				refreshPlayers()
			end
			if monsterLocator then
				task.defer(refreshMonsters)
			end
			setStatus("CHARACTER REFERENCES READY")
		else
			setStatus("CHARACTER REFRESH FAILED")
		end
	end)

	respawnButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
			loadCharacter(1)
		end

		if humanoid then
			humanoid.Health = 0
			setStatus("RESPAWNING")
		else
			setStatus("CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE")
		end
	end)

	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		if frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
		end
		frozen = false
		frozenRoot = nil
		setToggle(freezeButton,false,"FREEZE")
		restoreInvisible()
		invisibleEnabled = false
		gui:SetAttribute("InvisibleEnabled",false)
		setToggle(invisibleButton,false,"INVISIBLE")
		clearSelfHighlight()
		if selfHighlightEnabled then
			task.defer(function()
				if not menuDeleted then
					setSelfHighlight(true)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

	universalCleanup = function()
		liveHudEnabled = false
		if hud and hud.Parent then
			hud.Visible = false
		end

		if frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
		end
		frozen = false
		frozenRoot = nil

		if performanceMode then
			performanceMode = false
			restorePerformance()
		end

		if invisibleEnabled then
			invisibleEnabled = false
			restoreInvisible()
			gui:SetAttribute("InvisibleEnabled",false)
		end

		selfHighlightEnabled = false
		clearSelfHighlight()
		workspace.Gravity = originalGravity
		setToggle(hudButton,false,"LIVE HUD")
		setToggle(freezeButton,false,"FREEZE")
		setToggle(performanceButton,false,"PERFORMANCE")
		setToggle(invisibleButton,false,"INVISIBLE")
		setToggle(auraButton,false,"SELF AURA")
	end
end
safeSetup("Universal Extras",setupUniversalExtras)
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(otherPlayer)
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if not playerLocator then
		return
	end
	otherPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(.2)
		if playerLocator then
			refreshPlayers()
		end
	end)
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function()
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if playerLocator then
		task.defer(refreshPlayers)
	end
end)
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	stopFlight()
	restoreCollision()
	character = newCharacter
	humanoid =
		newCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		or newCharacter:WaitForChild("Humanoid",5)
	root =
		newCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		or newCharacter:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart",5)
	if not humanoid or not root then
		setStatus("CHARACTER REFERENCES INCOMPLETE")
		return
	end
	task.wait(.1)
	applyMovementStats()
	if playerLocator then
		refreshPlayers()
	end
	if monsterLocator then
		task.defer(refreshMonsters)
	end
end)

--============================================================
-- DRAGON REMAKE LABS
-- Navigator, accessibility and diagnostics are independent safe modules.
--============================================================
do
local function setupRemakeLabs()
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- NAVIGATOR
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		navigatorPage,
		"NAVIGATOR",
		"Live coordinates, heading, altitude and an optional compass HUD. Position tools never move your character."
	)

	local navigationCard = Instance.new("Frame")
	navigationCard.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,128)
	navigationCard.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	navigationCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
	navigationCard.Parent = navigatorPage
	corner(navigationCard,14)
	addStroke(navigationCard,accent,1,.55)

	local navigationGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	navigationGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,SURFACE_2),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,SURFACE)
	})
	navigationGradient.Rotation = 18
	navigationGradient.Parent = navigationCard

	local navigationTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	navigationTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	navigationTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,10)
	navigationTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,21)
	navigationTitle.Text = "LIVE POSITION"
	navigationTitle.TextSize = 10
	navigationTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	navigationTitle.TextColor3 = accent
	navigationTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	navigationTitle.Parent = navigationCard

	local navigationReadout = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	navigationReadout.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	navigationReadout.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,34)
	navigationReadout.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,1,-44)
	navigationReadout.Text = "X --   Y --   Z --\nHEADING --   SPEED --\nORIGIN DISTANCE --"
	navigationReadout.TextSize = 10
	navigationReadout.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	navigationReadout.TextColor3 = TEXT
	navigationReadout.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	navigationReadout.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	navigationReadout.TextWrapped = true
	navigationReadout.Parent = navigationCard

	local copyPositionButton,compassButton = makePair(
		navigatorPage,
		"COPY POSITION",
		"COMPASS HUD [OFF]"
	)
	local setOriginButton,clearOriginButton = makePair(
		navigatorPage,
		"SET ORIGIN",
		"CLEAR ORIGIN"
	)
	local copyPlaceButton,photoModeButton = makePair(
		navigatorPage,
		"COPY PLACE ID",
		"PHOTO MODE 5S"
	)

	makeSection(
		navigatorPage,
		"CAMERA QUICK TOOLS",
		"Switch perspective or restore the normal Roblox camera without changing your position."
	)

	local firstPersonButton,defaultCameraButton = makePair(
		navigatorPage,
		"FIRST PERSON [OFF]",
		"DEFAULT CAMERA"
	)

	local compassHud = Instance.new("Frame")
	compassHud.Name = "NotRubyCompassHUD"
	compassHud.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,0)
	compassHud.Position = UDim2.new(.5,0,0,14)
	compassHud.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(210,42)
	compassHud.BackgroundColor3 = PANEL
	compassHud.BackgroundTransparency = .08
	compassHud.BorderSizePixel = 0
	compassHud.Visible = false
	compassHud.ZIndex = 430
	compassHud.Parent = gui
	corner(compassHud,12)
	addStroke(compassHud,accent,1.4,.08)

	local compassText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	compassText.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	compassText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	compassText.Text = "N  •  000°"
	compassText.TextSize = 12
	compassText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	compassText.TextColor3 = TEXT
	compassText.ZIndex = 431
	compassText.Parent = compassHud

	local origin = nil
	local compassEnabled = false
	local firstPersonEnabled = false
	local photoModeActive = false
	local photoModeGeneration = 0
	local photoPanelWasVisible = false
	local photoMiniWasVisible = false
	local nextNavigationUpdate = 0

	local function headingData(look)
		local degrees =
			(math.deg(math.atan2(-look.X,-look.Z))+360)%360
		local names = {"N","NE","E","SE","S","SW","W","NW"}
		local index = math.floor((degrees+22.5)/45)%8+1
		return names[index],degrees
	end

	local function currentNavigationRoot()
		return
			root and root.Parent
			and root
			or (
				player.Character
				and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			)
	end

	local function copyText(value,successMessage)
		if type(setclipboard) ~= "function" then
			setStatus("CLIPBOARD UNAVAILABLE")
			return false
		end

		local ok = pcall(setclipboard,tostring(value))
		setStatus(ok and successMessage or "COPY FAILED")
		return ok
	end

	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if menuDeleted or os.clock() < nextNavigationUpdate then
			return
		end
		nextNavigationUpdate = os.clock()+.15

		if not navigatorPage.Visible and not compassEnabled then
			return
		end

		local currentRoot = currentNavigationRoot()
		if not currentRoot then
			navigationReadout.Text =
				"X --   Y --   Z --\nHEADING --   SPEED --\nORIGIN DISTANCE --"
			compassText.Text = "--  •  ---°"
			return
		end

		local position = currentRoot.Position
		local direction,degrees =
			headingData(currentRoot.CFrame.LookVector)
		local speed = currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
		local originText =
			origin
			and tostring(math.floor((position-origin).Magnitude+.5))
			or "NOT SET"

		navigationReadout.Text = string.format(
			"X %d   Y %d   Z %d\nHEADING %s / %03d°   SPEED %d\nORIGIN DISTANCE %s",
			math.floor(position.X+.5),
			math.floor(position.Y+.5),
			math.floor(position.Z+.5),
			direction,
			math.floor(degrees+.5)%360,
			math.floor(speed+.5),
			originText
		)
		compassText.Text =
			string.format("%s  •  %03d°",direction,math.floor(degrees+.5)%360)
	end)

	copyPositionButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local currentRoot = currentNavigationRoot()
		if not currentRoot then
			setStatus("CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE")
			return
		end

		local position = currentRoot.Position
		copyText(
			string.format("%.2f, %.2f, %.2f",position.X,position.Y,position.Z),
			"POSITION COPIED"
		)
	end)

	compassButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		compassEnabled = not compassEnabled
		compassHud.Visible = compassEnabled
		setToggle(compassButton,compassEnabled,"COMPASS HUD")
		setStatus(compassEnabled and "COMPASS HUD ON" or "COMPASS HUD OFF")
	end)

	setOriginButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local currentRoot = currentNavigationRoot()
		if not currentRoot then
			setStatus("CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE")
			return
		end
		origin = currentRoot.Position
		setStatus("NAVIGATION ORIGIN SET")
	end)

	clearOriginButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		origin = nil
		setStatus("NAVIGATION ORIGIN CLEARED")
	end)

	copyPlaceButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		copyText(tostring(game.PlaceId),"PLACE ID COPIED")
	end)

	photoModeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if menuDeleted or photoModeActive then
			if photoModeActive then
				setStatus("PHOTO MODE 5S")
			end
			return
		end

		photoModeActive = true
		photoModeGeneration += 1
		local generation = photoModeGeneration
		photoPanelWasVisible = panel.Visible
		photoMiniWasVisible = openButton.Visible
		local hudWasVisible = compassHud.Visible
		panel.Visible = false
		openButton.Visible = false
		compassHud.Visible = false

		task.delay(5,function()
			if menuDeleted
				or not gui.Parent
				or generation ~= photoModeGeneration then
				return
			end
			photoModeActive = false
			panel.Visible = photoPanelWasVisible
			openButton.Visible = photoMiniWasVisible
			compassHud.Visible = hudWasVisible and compassEnabled
			setStatus("PHOTO MODE FINISHED")
		end)
	end)

	firstPersonButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		firstPersonEnabled = not firstPersonEnabled
		player.CameraMode =
			firstPersonEnabled
			and Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson
			or Enum.CameraMode.Classic
		setToggle(firstPersonButton,firstPersonEnabled,"FIRST PERSON")
		setStatus(firstPersonEnabled and "FIRST PERSON ON" or "FIRST PERSON OFF")
	end)

	defaultCameraButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		firstPersonEnabled = false
		player.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.Classic
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		loadCharacter(1)
		if camera then
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			if humanoid then
				camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
			end
			camera.FieldOfView = originalFOV
		end
		setToggle(firstPersonButton,false,"FIRST PERSON")
		setStatus("DEFAULT CAMERA RESTORED")
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- ACCESSIBILITY
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		accessPage,
		"ACCESSIBILITY",
		"Readable text, higher contrast, calmer motion and scalable controls for mobile screens."
	)

	local largeTextButton,contrastButton = makePair(
		accessPage,
		"LARGE TEXT [OFF]",
		"HIGH CONTRAST [OFF]"
	)
	local reduceMotionButton,solidPanelsButton = makePair(
		accessPage,
		"REDUCE MOTION [OFF]",
		"SOLID PANELS [OFF]"
	)
	local scaleDownButton,scaleUpButton = makePair(
		accessPage,
		"UI SCALE -",
		"UI SCALE +"
	)
	local resetAccessButton = makeAction(accessPage,"RESET ACCESSIBILITY")

	makeSection(
		accessPage,
		"ACCESSIBILITY STATUS",
		"Changes apply immediately and can be reset together."
	)

	local largeTextEnabled = false
	local highContrastEnabled = false
	local solidPanelsEnabled = false
	local reduceMotionEnabled = false
	local accessibilityScale =
		tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale")) or 1

	local function rememberTextObject(object)
		if object:GetAttribute("NotRubyBaseTextSize") == nil then
			object:SetAttribute("NotRubyBaseTextSize",object.TextSize)
		end
		if object:GetAttribute("NotRubyBaseTextColor") == nil then
			object:SetAttribute("NotRubyBaseTextColor",object.TextColor3)
		end
	end

	local function applyAccessibilityToObject(object)
		if not object or not object.Parent then
			return
		end

		if object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton")
			or object:IsA("TextBox") then
			rememberTextObject(object)
			local baseSize =
				tonumber(object:GetAttribute("NotRubyBaseTextSize"))
				or object.TextSize
			object.TextSize =
				largeTextEnabled
				and math.clamp(math.floor(baseSize*1.18+.5),8,24)
				or baseSize

			local baseColor =
				object:GetAttribute("NotRubyBaseTextColor")
			object.TextColor3 =
				highContrastEnabled
				and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
				or (
					baseColor ~= nil
					and baseColor
					or TEXT
				)
		end

		if object:IsA("GuiObject")
			and object.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then
			if object:GetAttribute("NotRubyBaseBackgroundTransparency") == nil then
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyBaseBackgroundTransparency",
					object.BackgroundTransparency
				)
			end
			local base =
				tonumber(
					object:GetAttribute("NotRubyBaseBackgroundTransparency")
				)
				or object.BackgroundTransparency
			object.BackgroundTransparency =
				solidPanelsEnabled and math.min(base,.03) or base
		end
	end

	local function applyAccessibility()
		gui:SetAttribute("ReduceMotion",reduceMotionEnabled)
		for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
			applyAccessibilityToObject(object)
		end

		local glow = header:FindFirstChild("AnimatedAccentGlow")
		if glow then
			glow.Visible = not reduceMotionEnabled
		end

		local dragonBadge = header:FindFirstChild("DragonBadge")
		local pulse =
			dragonBadge
			and dragonBadge:FindFirstChild("BrandPulseScale")
		if brandPulseTween then
			if reduceMotionEnabled then
				brandPulseTween:Pause()
			else
				brandPulseTween:Play()
			end
		end
		if pulse and pulse:IsA("UIScale") and reduceMotionEnabled then
			pulse.Scale = 1
		end
	end

	largeTextButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		largeTextEnabled = not largeTextEnabled
		setToggle(largeTextButton,largeTextEnabled,"LARGE TEXT")
		applyAccessibility()
		setStatus(largeTextEnabled and "LARGE TEXT ON" or "LARGE TEXT OFF")
	end)

	contrastButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		highContrastEnabled = not highContrastEnabled
		setToggle(contrastButton,highContrastEnabled,"HIGH CONTRAST")
		applyAccessibility()
		setStatus(
			highContrastEnabled
			and "HIGH CONTRAST ON"
			or "HIGH CONTRAST OFF"
		)
	end)

	reduceMotionButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		reduceMotionEnabled = not reduceMotionEnabled
		setToggle(reduceMotionButton,reduceMotionEnabled,"REDUCE MOTION")
		applyAccessibility()
		setStatus(
			reduceMotionEnabled
			and "REDUCE MOTION ON"
			or "REDUCE MOTION OFF"
		)
	end)

	solidPanelsButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		solidPanelsEnabled = not solidPanelsEnabled
		setToggle(solidPanelsButton,solidPanelsEnabled,"SOLID PANELS")
		applyAccessibility()
		setStatus(
			solidPanelsEnabled
			and "SOLID PANELS ON"
			or "SOLID PANELS OFF"
		)
	end)

	local function applyAccessibilityScale(value)
		accessibilityScale = setPanelScale(value,true)
		setStatus(
			"UI SCALE "
			..tostring(math.floor(accessibilityScale*100+.5))
			.."%"
		)
	end

	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("PanelScale"):Connect(function()
		accessibilityScale =
			tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("PanelScale")) or 1
	end)

	scaleDownButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		applyAccessibilityScale(accessibilityScale-.1)
	end)

	scaleUpButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		applyAccessibilityScale(accessibilityScale+.1)
	end)

	resetAccessButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		largeTextEnabled = false
		highContrastEnabled = false
		solidPanelsEnabled = false
		reduceMotionEnabled = false
		setToggle(largeTextButton,false,"LARGE TEXT")
		setToggle(contrastButton,false,"HIGH CONTRAST")
		setToggle(reduceMotionButton,false,"REDUCE MOTION")
		setToggle(solidPanelsButton,false,"SOLID PANELS")
		applyAccessibilityScale(1)
		applyAccessibility()
		setStatus("ACCESSIBILITY RESET")
	end)

	gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
		if not (
			largeTextEnabled
			or highContrastEnabled
			or solidPanelsEnabled
		) then
			return
		end
		task.defer(function()
			if object and object.Parent then
				applyAccessibilityToObject(object)
			end
		end)
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- SYSTEM LAB
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		systemPage,
		"SYSTEM LAB",
		"Run live health checks, repair references and create a copyable diagnostic report."
	)

	local systemCard = Instance.new("Frame")
	systemCard.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,190)
	systemCard.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	systemCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
	systemCard.Parent = systemPage
	corner(systemCard,14)
	addStroke(systemCard,accent,1,.58)

	local systemText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	systemText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	systemText.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,12)
	systemText.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,1,-24)
	systemText.Text = "SELF TEST HAS NOT RUN"
	systemText.TextSize = 9
	systemText.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	systemText.TextColor3 = TEXT
	systemText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	systemText.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	systemText.TextWrapped = true
	systemText.Parent = systemCard

	local runTestButton,repairRefsButton = makePair(
		systemPage,
		"RUN SELF TEST",
		"REPAIR REFERENCES"
	)
	local copyReportButton,cleanVisualsButton = makePair(
		systemPage,
		"COPY REPORT",
		"CLEAN TEMP VISUALS"
	)
	local recoverUiButton = makeAction(systemPage,"RECOVER UI LAYOUT")

	local lastReport = "SELF TEST HAS NOT RUN"

	local function tableCount(value)
		local count = 0
		for _ in pairs(value) do
			count += 1
		end
		return count
	end

	local function yesNo(value)
		return value and "OK" or "MISSING"
	end

	local function runSelfTest()
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local characterReady = loadCharacter(0)
		local fileStorage =
			type(isfile) == "function"
			and type(readfile) == "function"
			and type(writefile) == "function"
		local viewport =
			camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.zero

		lastReport = table.concat({
			"NOT RUBY V1 // DRAGON REMAKE",
			"GUI: "..yesNo(gui and gui.Parent),
			"PANEL: "..yesNo(panel and panel.Parent),
			"CAMERA: "..yesNo(camera),
			"CHARACTER: "..yesNo(characterReady),
			"HUMANOID: "..yesNo(humanoid and humanoid.Parent),
			"ROOT: "..yesNo(root and root.Parent),
			"PAGES: "..tostring(tableCount(pages)),
			"TABS: "..tostring(tableCount(tabButtons)),
			"CURRENT TAB: "..tostring(currentTab),
			"STARTUP ISSUES: "..tostring(#startupIssues),
			#startupIssues > 0
				and "ISSUE DETAILS: "..table.concat(startupIssues," | ")
				or "ISSUE DETAILS: NONE",
			"PLAYER MARKERS: "..tostring(tableCount(playerMarkers)),
			"CREATURE MARKERS: "..tostring(tableCount(monsterMarkers)),
			"FILE STORAGE: "..(fileStorage and "AVAILABLE" or "SESSION ONLY"),
			"VIEWPORT: "
				..tostring(math.floor(viewport.X))
				.." x "
				..tostring(math.floor(viewport.Y)),
			"PLACE ID: "..tostring(game.PlaceId)
		},"\n")

		systemText.Text = lastReport
		setStatus(
			characterReady
			and "SELF TEST PASSED"
			or "SELF TEST // CHARACTER NOT READY"
		)
	end

	runTestButton.Activated:Connect(runSelfTest)

	repairRefsButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if loadCharacter(3) then
			applyMovementStats()
			camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			setStatus("REFERENCES REPAIRED")
		else
			setStatus("REFERENCE REPAIR FAILED")
		end
		runSelfTest()
	end)

	copyReportButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if type(setclipboard) ~= "function" then
			setStatus("CLIPBOARD UNAVAILABLE")
			return
		end
		local ok = pcall(setclipboard,lastReport)
		setStatus(ok and "DIAGNOSTIC REPORT COPIED" or "COPY FAILED")
	end)

	cleanVisualsButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		gui:SetAttribute("WallPartsVisual",false)
		setPlayerLocator(false)
		setMonsterLocator(false)
		clearMarkerTable(playerMarkers)
		clearMarkerTable(monsterMarkers)

		for _,object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if object.Name == "NotRubySparkBurst" then
				object:Destroy()
			end
		end
		setStatus("TEMP VISUALS CLEANED")
		runSelfTest()
	end)

	recoverUiButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport =
			camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
		local width = math.clamp(620,340,math.max(340,viewport.X-24))
		local height = math.clamp(460,300,math.max(300,viewport.Y-44))
		panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width,height)
		panel.Position = UDim2.new(.5,-width*.5,.5,-height*.5)
		panel.Visible = true
		openButton.Visible = false
		setPanelScale(1,false)
		setStatus("UI LAYOUT RECOVERED")
	end)

	remakeLabsCleanup = function()
		photoModeGeneration += 1
		if photoModeActive then
			photoModeActive = false
			panel.Visible = photoPanelWasVisible
			openButton.Visible = photoMiniWasVisible
		end
		compassEnabled = false
		compassHud.Visible = false
		origin = nil
		firstPersonEnabled = false
		player.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.Classic
		setToggle(compassButton,false,"COMPASS HUD")
		setToggle(firstPersonButton,false,"FIRST PERSON")

		largeTextEnabled = false
		highContrastEnabled = false
		solidPanelsEnabled = false
		reduceMotionEnabled = false
		setToggle(largeTextButton,false,"LARGE TEXT")
		setToggle(contrastButton,false,"HIGH CONTRAST")
		setToggle(reduceMotionButton,false,"REDUCE MOTION")
		setToggle(solidPanelsButton,false,"SOLID PANELS")
		applyAccessibilityScale(1)
		applyAccessibility()

		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if camera then
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
				camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
			end
		end
	end

	task.defer(runSelfTest)
end
safeSetup("Remake Labs",setupRemakeLabs)
end

do
local function setupPowerSuite()
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

	local state = {
		file = "not_ruby_v1_dragon_"..tostring(player.UserId)..".json",
		canFiles =
			type(isfile) == "function"
			and type(readfile) == "function"
			and type(writefile) == "function",
		data = {
			favorites = {},
			keybinds = {
				Flight = "F",
				Noclip = "N",
				Invisible = "X",
				WallParts = "V"
			},
			waypoints = {},
			presets = {},
			settings = {}
		},
		registry = {},
		waypointObjects = {},
		stats = {
			started = os.clock(),
			distance = 0,
			maxSpeed = 0,
			jumps = 0,
			fps = 0,
			minFps = 999,
			lastPosition = nil
		}
	}

	local ui = {}

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Storage
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local function loadStorage()
		if not state.canFiles then
			return
		end

		pcall(function()
			if not isfile(state.file) then
				return
			end

			local decoded =
				HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(state.file))

			if typeof(decoded) ~= "table" then
				return
			end

			for key,value in pairs(decoded) do
				state.data[key] = value
			end

			state.data.favorites =
				typeof(state.data.favorites) == "table"
				and state.data.favorites
				or {}
			state.data.keybinds =
				typeof(state.data.keybinds) == "table"
				and state.data.keybinds
				or {}
			state.data.waypoints =
				typeof(state.data.waypoints) == "table"
				and state.data.waypoints
				or {}
			state.data.presets =
				typeof(state.data.presets) == "table"
				and state.data.presets
				or {}
			state.data.settings =
				typeof(state.data.settings) == "table"
				and state.data.settings
				or {}

			local defaults = {
				Flight = "F",
				Noclip = "N",
				Invisible = "X",
				WallParts = "V"
			}

			for feature,keyName in pairs(defaults) do
				if type(state.data.keybinds[feature]) ~= "string" then
					state.data.keybinds[feature] = keyName
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function saveStorage()
		if not state.canFiles then
			return false
		end

		local ok = pcall(function()
			writefile(
				state.file,
				HttpService:JSONEncode(state.data)
			)
		end)

		return ok
	end

	loadStorage()

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Error notification sound system
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local errorSoundOptions = {
		{name="RUBY ALERT",id="85552330273422"},
		{name="VOID PULSE",id="79908084264003"},
		{name="SYSTEM GLITCH",id="134254842665299"},
		{name="CRITICAL SIGNAL",id="91681144991126"},
		{name="NEON WARNING",id="131447940085093"},
		{name="EXECUTOR ALERT",id="92466756400564"},

		-- Public notification sounds found in Roblox Creator Store.
		{name="QUICK NOTIFICATION",id="7383525713"},
		{name="SHORT NOTICE",id="18595195017"},
		{name="NOTIFICATION BEEP",id="6176999962"}
	}

	local errorSoundEnabled =
		state.data.settings.errorSoundEnabled ~= false
	local errorSoundIndex =
		math.clamp(
			math.floor(
				tonumber(state.data.settings.errorSoundIndex) or 1
			),
			1,
			#errorSoundOptions
		)

	local customErrorSoundId =
		tostring(state.data.settings.customErrorSoundId or "")
			:gsub("%D","")

	local useCustomErrorSound =
		state.data.settings.useCustomErrorSound == true
		and customErrorSoundId ~= ""

	local function currentErrorSoundId()
		if useCustomErrorSound and customErrorSoundId ~= "" then
			return customErrorSoundId
		end

		return errorSoundOptions[errorSoundIndex].id
	end

	local function currentErrorSoundName()
		if useCustomErrorSound and customErrorSoundId ~= "" then
			return "CUSTOM "..customErrorSoundId
		end

		return errorSoundOptions[errorSoundIndex].name
	end

	local errorSoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
	local errorContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
	local errorSound = Instance.new("Sound")
	errorSound.Name = "NotRubyErrorSound"
	errorSound.Volume = 1
	errorSound.PlaybackSpeed = 1
	errorSound.SoundId =
		"rbxassetid://"..currentErrorSoundId()
	errorSound.Parent = errorSoundService
	local lastErrorSoundAt = 0
	local lastErrorToastText = ""
	local lastErrorToastAt = 0

	gui:SetAttribute("ErrorSoundEnabled",errorSoundEnabled)
	gui:SetAttribute(
		"ErrorSoundId",
		currentErrorSoundId()
	)

	local function playErrorSound(forcePreview)
		if not forcePreview and not errorSoundEnabled then
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		if not forcePreview and now-lastErrorSoundAt < .65 then
			return
		end
		lastErrorSoundAt = now

		errorSound.SoundId =
			"rbxassetid://"..currentErrorSoundId()

		task.spawn(function()
			pcall(function()
				errorContentProvider:PreloadAsync({errorSound})
			end)

			if not errorSound or not errorSound.Parent then
				return
			end

			pcall(function()
				errorSound:Stop()
				errorSound.TimePosition = 0
			end)

			local played =
				pcall(function()
					errorSoundService:PlayLocalSound(errorSound)
				end)

			if not played then
				pcall(function()
					errorSound:Play()
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function isErrorNotification(message)
		local upper = string.upper(tostring(message or ""))

		local patterns = {
			"INVALID",
			"FAILED",
			"UNAVAILABLE",
			"NOT FOUND",
			"NO PLAYER",
			"NO CHARACTER",
			"PRESET SLOT IS EMPTY",
			"FILE STORAGE UNAVAILABLE",
			"CLIPBOARD UNAVAILABLE",
			"COPY FAILED",
			"LANGUAGE NOT FOUND",
			"NO FEATURE FOUND",
			"NO COUNTRY FOUND",
			"SELECT A PLAYER FIRST",
			"LOCATOR OFF"
		}

		for _,pattern in ipairs(patterns) do
			if string.find(upper,pattern,1,true) then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Animated notification system
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local notifyEvent = gui:FindFirstChild("NotRubyNotifyEvent")
	if not notifyEvent then
		notifyEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		notifyEvent.Name = "NotRubyNotifyEvent"
		notifyEvent.Parent = gui
	end

	ui.toastHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
	ui.toastHolder.Name = "NotRubyToasts"
	ui.toastHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
	ui.toastHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1,-12,0,12)
	ui.toastHolder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300,360)
	ui.toastHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ui.toastHolder.ZIndex = 1200
	ui.toastHolder.Parent = gui

	local toastLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	toastLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,6)
	toastLayout.HorizontalAlignment =
		Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
	toastLayout.VerticalAlignment =
		Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
	toastLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	toastLayout.Parent = ui.toastHolder

	local function notify(message)
		if menuDeleted or not ui.toastHolder.Parent then
			return
		end

		message = tostring(message or "")
		local errorMode = isErrorNotification(message)
		local now = os.clock()

		-- Repeated frame-level failures should not flood the screen or audio.
		if errorMode
			and message == lastErrorToastText
			and now-lastErrorToastAt < 1.15 then
			return
		end

		if errorMode then
			lastErrorToastText = message
			lastErrorToastAt = now
			playErrorSound(false)
		end

		local toast = Instance.new("Frame")
		toast.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(286,48)
		toast.BackgroundColor3 =
			errorMode
			and Color3.fromRGB(68,18,28)
			or PANEL
		toast.BackgroundTransparency = .04
		toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
		toast.ZIndex = 1201
		toast.Parent = ui.toastHolder
		corner(toast,11)
		addStroke(
			toast,
			errorMode
			and Color3.fromRGB(255,75,96)
			or (gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent),
			errorMode and 1.7 or 1.2,
			.04
		)

		local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
		scale.Scale = .82
		scale.Parent = toast

		if errorMode then
			local errorMark = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			errorMark.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			errorMark.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9,5)
			errorMark.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24,38)
			errorMark.Text = "!"
			errorMark.TextSize = 17
			errorMark.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
			errorMark.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,92,108)
			errorMark.ZIndex = 1202
			errorMark.Parent = toast
		end

		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Position =
			errorMode
			and UDim2.fromOffset(34,6)
			or UDim2.fromOffset(11,6)
		label.Size =
			errorMode
			and UDim2.new(1,-44,1,-12)
			or UDim2.new(1,-22,1,-12)
		label.Text = message
		label.TextSize = 9
		label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		label.TextColor3 = TEXT
		label.TextWrapped = true
		label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		label.ZIndex = 1202
		label.Parent = toast

		TweenService:Create(
			scale,
			TweenInfo.new(
				.16,
				Enum.EasingStyle.Back,
				Enum.EasingDirection.Out
			),
			{Scale = 1}
		):Play()

		task.delay(errorMode and 3.2 or 2.6,function()
			if not toast.Parent then
				return
			end

			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.14),
				{Scale = .82}
			):Play()
			TweenService:Create(
				toast,
				TweenInfo.new(.14),
				{BackgroundTransparency = 1}
			):Play()

			task.wait(.15)
			if toast.Parent then
				toast:Destroy()
			end
		end)
	end

	notifyEvent.Event:Connect(notify)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Search + Favorites
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local function cleanLabel(value)
		value = tostring(value or "")
		value = string.gsub(value,"%s+%[ON%]","")
		value = string.gsub(value,"%s+%[OFF%]","")
		value = string.gsub(value,"%s+"," ")
		return value
	end

	local function jumpToFeature(entry)
		if not entry
			or not entry.button
			or not entry.button.Parent then
			return
		end

		local page = pages[entry.page]
		if not page then
			return
		end

		setTab(entry.page)

		task.defer(function()
			task.wait()
			local relative =
				entry.button.AbsolutePosition.Y
				- page.AbsolutePosition.Y
				+ page.CanvasPosition.Y
				- 55

			page.CanvasPosition =
				Vector2.new(0,math.max(0,relative))

			local scale =
				entry.button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")

			if scale then
				scale.Scale = .92
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(
						.22,
						Enum.EasingStyle.Back,
						Enum.EasingDirection.Out
					),
					{Scale = 1}
				):Play()
			end
		end)
	end

	local function clearChildren(frame)
		for _,child in ipairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
			if not child:IsA("UIListLayout")
				and not child:IsA("UIPadding") then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end

	local rebuildFavorites

	local function makeFavoriteStar(button,pageName)
		if button:GetAttribute("NotRubyFavoriteStarred") then
			return
		end

		if button.Name == "FavoriteStar"
			or button.Text == ""
			or button:IsDescendantOf(ui.favoritesPage)
			or button:IsDescendantOf(ui.waypointsPage)
			or button:IsDescendantOf(ui.statsPage) then
			return
		end

		local label = cleanLabel(button.Text)
		if label == "" then
			return
		end

		local key = pageName.."|"..label

		button:SetAttribute("NotRubyFavoriteStarred",true)
		button:SetAttribute("NotRubyFeatureKey",key)

		state.registry[key] = {
			button = button,
			page = pageName,
			label = label
		}

		local star = Instance.new("TextButton")
		star.Name = "FavoriteStar"
		star.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
		star.Position = UDim2.new(1,-3,0,3)
		star.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(21,21)
		star.Text =
			state.data.favorites[key]
			and "★"
			or "☆"
		star.TextSize = 13
		star.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		star.TextColor3 = accent
		star.BackgroundColor3 = PANEL
		star.BackgroundTransparency = .14
		star.BorderSizePixel = 0
		star.AutoButtonColor = false
		star.ZIndex = button.ZIndex + 10
		star.Parent = button
		corner(star,7)

		star.Activated:Connect(function()
			if state.data.favorites[key] then
				state.data.favorites[key] = nil
				star.Text = "☆"
				notify("REMOVED FROM FAVORITES // "..label)
			else
				state.data.favorites[key] = true
				star.Text = "★"
				notify("ADDED TO FAVORITES // "..label)
			end

			saveStorage()
			if rebuildFavorites then
				rebuildFavorites()
			end
		end)
	end

	local function scanFeatureButtons()
		local excluded = {
			FAVORITES = true,
			WAYPOINTS = true,
			STATS = true
		}

		for pageName,page in pairs(pages) do
			if not excluded[pageName] then
				for _,object in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
					if object:IsA("TextButton")
						and object.Name ~= "FavoriteStar"
						and not object.Parent:IsA("TextButton") then
						makeFavoriteStar(object,pageName)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	rebuildFavorites = function()
		clearChildren(ui.favoriteResults)

		local count = 0
		for key,enabled in pairs(state.data.favorites) do
			local entry = state.registry[key]

			if enabled and entry
				and entry.button
				and entry.button.Parent then

				count += 1
				local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
				button.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,42)
				button.Text =
					"★  ["
					..entry.page
					.."]  "
					..entry.label
				button.TextSize = 9
				button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				button.TextColor3 = TEXT
				button.TextXAlignment =
					Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
				button.BorderSizePixel = 0
				button.AutoButtonColor = false
				button.Parent = ui.favoriteResults
				corner(button,10)

				local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
				padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,12)
				padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,8)
				padding.Parent = button

				button.Activated:Connect(function()
					jumpToFeature(entry)
				end)
			end
		end

		if count == 0 then
			local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			empty.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,50)
			empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			empty.Text =
				"NO FAVORITES YET // TAP ☆ ON A FEATURE"
			empty.TextSize = 9
			empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			empty.TextColor3 = MUTED
			empty.Parent = ui.favoriteResults
		end
	end

	local function rebuildSearch()
		clearChildren(ui.searchResults)

		local query =
			string.lower(
				string.gsub(
					ui.searchBox.Text or "",
					"^%s*(.-)%s*$",
					"%1"
				)
			)

		if query == "" then
			ui.searchResults.Visible = false
			ui.searchResults.Size = UDim2.new(1,-147,0,0)
			return
		end

		local matches = {}

		for _,entry in pairs(state.registry) do
			local haystack =
				string.lower(
					entry.label.." "..entry.page
				)

			if string.find(haystack,query,1,true) then
				table.insert(matches,entry)
			end
		end

		table.sort(matches,function(a,b)
			return a.label < b.label
		end)

		local shown = math.min(#matches,6)

		ui.searchResults.Visible = true
		ui.searchResults.Size =
			UDim2.new(
				1,
				-147,
				0,
				shown > 0 and (shown*41 + 10) or 54
			)

		for index,entry in ipairs(matches) do
			if index > 24 then
				break
			end

			local result = Instance.new("TextButton")
			result.Size = UDim2.new(1,-10,0,36)
			result.Text =
				"["..entry.page.."]  "..entry.label
			result.TextSize = 9
			result.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			result.TextColor3 = TEXT
			result.TextXAlignment =
				Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			result.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
			result.BorderSizePixel = 0
			result.AutoButtonColor = false
			result.LayoutOrder = index
			result.ZIndex = 251
			result.Parent = ui.searchResults
			corner(result,9)

			local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
			padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,11)
			padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,8)
			padding.Parent = result

			result.Activated:Connect(function()
				ui.searchBox.Text = ""
				ui.searchResults.Visible = false
				jumpToFeature(entry)
			end)
		end

		if #matches == 0 then
			local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			empty.Size = UDim2.new(1,-10,0,40)
			empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			empty.Text = "NO FEATURE FOUND"
			empty.TextSize = 9
			empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			empty.TextColor3 = MUTED
			empty.ZIndex = 251
			empty.Parent = ui.searchResults
		end
	end

	ui.searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(
		rebuildSearch
	)

	ui.searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		if ui.searchBox.Text == "" then
			ui.searchResults.Visible = false
		end
	end)

	ui.waypointFolder.Name = "NotRubyWaypoints"
	ui.waypointFolder.Parent = workspace

	local rebuildWaypoints

	local function destroyWaypointObjects()
		for _,object in pairs(state.waypointObjects) do
			if object and object.Parent then
				object:Destroy()
			end
		end
		table.clear(state.waypointObjects)
	end

	local function makeWaypointObject(index,waypoint)
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Name = "Waypoint_"..tostring(index)
		part.Size = Vector3.new(.2,.2,.2)
		part.Anchored = true
		part.CanCollide = false
		part.CanTouch = false
		part.CanQuery = false
		part.Transparency = 1
		part.Position = Vector3.new(
			tonumber(waypoint.x) or 0,
			tonumber(waypoint.y) or 0,
			tonumber(waypoint.z) or 0
		)
		part.Parent = ui.waypointFolder

		local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		billboard.Name = "WaypointBillboard"
		billboard.Adornee = part
		billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220,48)
		billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,2.5,0)
		billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
		billboard.Parent = part

		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.Name = "Label"
		label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Text =
			"◆ "
			..tostring(waypoint.name or ("Waypoint "..index))
		label.TextSize = 10
		label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		label.TextColor3 = accent
		label.TextStrokeTransparency = .25
		label.Parent = billboard

		state.waypointObjects[index] = part
	end

	rebuildWaypoints = function()
		clearChildren(ui.waypointList)
		destroyWaypointObjects()

		for index,waypoint in ipairs(state.data.waypoints) do
			makeWaypointObject(index,waypoint)

			local row = Instance.new("Frame")
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,48)
			row.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
			row.BorderSizePixel = 0
			row.Parent = ui.waypointList
			corner(row,10)

			local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10,5)
			label.Size = UDim2.new(1,-92,1,-10)
			label.Text =
				tostring(waypoint.name or ("Waypoint "..index))
				.."  //  "
				..math.floor(tonumber(waypoint.x) or 0)
				..", "
				..math.floor(tonumber(waypoint.y) or 0)
				..", "
				..math.floor(tonumber(waypoint.z) or 0)
			label.TextSize = 8
			label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			label.TextColor3 = TEXT
			label.TextWrapped = true
			label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			label.Parent = row

			local deleteButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
			deleteButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,.5)
			deleteButton.Position = UDim2.new(1,-7,.5,0)
			deleteButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72,32)
			deleteButton.Text = "DELETE"
			deleteButton.TextSize = 8
			deleteButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
			deleteButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
			deleteButton.BackgroundColor3 =
				Color3.fromRGB(92,32,43)
			deleteButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
			deleteButton.AutoButtonColor = false
			deleteButton.Parent = row
			corner(deleteButton,8)

			deleteButton.Activated:Connect(function()
				table.remove(state.data.waypoints,index)
				saveStorage()
				rebuildWaypoints()
				notify("WAYPOINT DELETED")
			end)
		end

		if #state.data.waypoints == 0 then
			local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			empty.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
			empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			empty.Text = "NO WAYPOINTS SAVED"
			empty.TextSize = 9
			empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			empty.TextColor3 = MUTED
			empty.Parent = ui.waypointList
		end
	end

	ui.saveWaypoint.Activated:Connect(function()
		local currentRoot =
			player.Character
			and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not currentRoot then
			notify("NO CHARACTER ROOT FOUND")
			return
		end

		if #state.data.waypoints >= 30 then
			notify("WAYPOINT LIMIT REACHED // 30")
			return
		end

		local name =
			string.gsub(
				ui.waypointName.Text or "",
				"^%s*(.-)%s*$",
				"%1"
			)

		if name == "" then
			name =
				"WAYPOINT "
				..tostring(#state.data.waypoints + 1)
		end

		table.insert(
			state.data.waypoints,
			{
				name = name,
				x = currentRoot.Position.X,
				y = currentRoot.Position.Y,
				z = currentRoot.Position.Z
			}
		)

		ui.waypointName.Text = ""
		saveStorage()
		rebuildWaypoints()
		notify("WAYPOINT SAVED // "..name)
	end)

	ui.clearWaypoints.Activated:Connect(function()
		table.clear(state.data.waypoints)
		saveStorage()
		rebuildWaypoints()
		notify("ALL WAYPOINTS CLEARED")
	end)

	rebuildWaypoints()

	-- Live distance on waypoint markers.
	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent and not menuDeleted do
			local currentRoot =
				player.Character
				and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if currentRoot then
				for index,part in pairs(state.waypointObjects) do
					local waypoint = state.data.waypoints[index]
					local billboard =
						part
						and part:FindFirstChild("WaypointBillboard")
					local label =
						billboard
						and billboard:FindFirstChild("Label")

					if waypoint and label then
						local distance =
							math.floor(
								(currentRoot.Position-part.Position)
								.Magnitude
							)

						label.Text =
							"◆ "
							..tostring(waypoint.name)
							.." // "
							..tostring(distance)
							.." studs"
						label.TextColor3 = accent
					end
				end
			end

			task.wait(.35)
		end
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Session stats
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		ui.statsPage,
		"SESSION STATS",
		"Live session information: time, distance, speed, jumps and FPS."
	)

	local function makeStatRow(titleText)
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
		frame.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
		frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame.Parent = ui.statsPage
		corner(frame,10)

		local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11,0)
		title.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-12,1,0)
		title.Text = titleText
		title.TextSize = 9
		title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		title.TextColor3 = MUTED
		title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		title.Parent = frame

		local value = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		value.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		value.Position = UDim2.new(.5,0,0,0)
		value.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-11,1,0)
		value.Text = "--"
		value.TextSize = 10
		value.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		value.TextColor3 = TEXT
		value.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
		value.Parent = frame

		return value
	end

	ui.statTime = makeStatRow("TIME PLAYED")
	ui.statDistance = makeStatRow("DISTANCE MOVED")
	ui.statSpeed = makeStatRow("CURRENT / MAX SPEED")
	ui.statJumps = makeStatRow("JUMPS")
	ui.statFps = makeStatRow("FPS / MIN FPS")
	ui.statPlayers = makeStatRow("PLAYERS")

	local fpsFrames = 0
	local fpsStarted = os.clock()
	local humanoidConnection

	local function connectStatsHumanoid()
		if humanoidConnection then
			humanoidConnection:Disconnect()
			humanoidConnection = nil
		end

		local currentHumanoid =
			player.Character
			and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if currentHumanoid then
			humanoidConnection =
				currentHumanoid.StateChanged:Connect(
					function(_,newState)
						if newState
							== Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
							state.stats.jumps += 1
						end
					end
				)
		end
	end

	connectStatsHumanoid()

	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		state.stats.lastPosition = nil
		task.wait(.2)
		connectStatsHumanoid()
	end)

	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		fpsFrames += 1

		local now = os.clock()
		local elapsed = now - fpsStarted

		if elapsed >= .5 then
			state.stats.fps =
				math.floor(fpsFrames / elapsed + .5)

			if state.stats.fps > 0 then
				state.stats.minFps =
					math.min(
						state.stats.minFps,
						state.stats.fps
					)
			end

			fpsFrames = 0
			fpsStarted = now
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent and not menuDeleted do
			local currentRoot =
				player.Character
				and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if currentRoot then
				local position = currentRoot.Position
				local speed =
					Vector3.new(
						currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
						0,
						currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
					).Magnitude

				state.stats.maxSpeed =
					math.max(state.stats.maxSpeed,speed)

				if state.stats.lastPosition then
					local delta =
						(position-state.stats.lastPosition).Magnitude

					-- Ignore huge teleports when counting traveled distance.
					if delta < 120 then
						state.stats.distance += delta
					end
				end

				state.stats.lastPosition = position

				local seconds =
					math.floor(os.clock()-state.stats.started)
				local hours = math.floor(seconds/3600)
				local minutes =
					math.floor((seconds%3600)/60)
				local secs = seconds%60

				ui.statTime.Text =
					string.format(
						"%02d:%02d:%02d",
						hours,
						minutes,
						secs
					)
				ui.statDistance.Text =
					tostring(math.floor(state.stats.distance))
					.." studs"
				ui.statSpeed.Text =
					tostring(math.floor(speed+.5))
					.." / "
					..tostring(math.floor(state.stats.maxSpeed+.5))
				ui.statJumps.Text =
					tostring(state.stats.jumps)
				ui.statFps.Text =
					tostring(state.stats.fps)
					.." / "
					..(
						state.stats.minFps == 999
						and "--"
						or tostring(state.stats.minFps)
					)
				ui.statPlayers.Text =
					tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
			end

			task.wait(.2)
		end
	end)

			["INVALID WALK SPEED"] = "НЕКОРРЕКТНАЯ СКОРОСТЬ ХОДЬБЫ",

			["NO CHARACTER ROOT FOUND"] = "КОРНЕВАЯ ЧАСТЬ ПЕРСОНАЖА НЕ НАЙДЕНА",

			["PERFORMANCE MODE"] = "РЕЖИМ ПРОИЗВОДИТЕЛЬНОСТИ",
			["PHOTO MODE FINISHED"] = "ФОТОРЕЖИМ ЗАВЕРШЁН",
			["PLAYERS REFRESHED"] = "ИГРОКИ ОБНОВЛЕНЫ",
			["POSITION COPIED"] = "ПОЗИЦИЯ СКОПИРОВАНА",

			["SELF TEST"] = "САМОПРОВЕРКА",

			["TYPE A LANGUAGE OR COUNTRY"] = "ВВЕДИТЕ ЯЗЫК ИЛИ СТРАНУ",

			["UI FIT TO SCREEN"] = "UI ПОДОГНАН ПОД ЭКРАН",
			["UI LAYOUT RECOVERED"] = "РАСКЛАДКА UI ВОССТАНОВЛЕНА",
			["UNAVAILABLE"] = "НЕДОСТУПНО",
			["VISUALS RESET"] = "ВИЗУАЛ СБРОШЕН",
			["WALL PARTS"] = "ЧАСТИ СТЕН",
			["WAYPOINT DELETED"] = "ТОЧКА УДАЛЕНА",
			["WAYPOINT LIMIT REACHED"] = "ДОСТИГНУТ ЛИМИТ ТОЧЕК",
			["WAYPOINT SAVED"] = "ТОЧКА СОХРАНЕНА",
			["WELCOME RECOVERED"] = "ЭКРАН ПРИВЕТСТВИЯ ВОССТАНОВЛЕН",

STRICT BASEPLATE MODE is for simple games and also watches replicated physics spikes, force objects, abnormal jumps, noclip-like paths, flight and teleports."] = "Выберите игрока. 			,["Server labels recognized: Delta, Wave, Arceus X, Codex, Fluxus and Hydrogen. Other reported names are also shown."] = "Показываются серверные метки, включая Delta, Wave, Arceus X, Codex, Fluxus и Hydrogen."
			,["repeated position jumps"] = "ПОВТОРЯЮЩИЕСЯ СКАЧКИ ПОЗИЦИИ"

			,["repeated upward flight-like movement"] = "ПОВТОРЯЮЩЕЕСЯ ДВИЖЕНИЕ ВВЕРХ"
			,["extreme replicated physics spike"] = "РЕЗКИЙ СКАЧОК СЕТЕВОЙ ФИЗИКИ"
			,["character force / mover object"] = "СИЛОВОЙ ОБЪЕКТ В ПЕРСОНАЖЕ"
			,["abnormal jump setting"] = "НЕОБЫЧНАЯ НАСТРОЙКА ПРЫЖКА"
			,["possible noclip through solid parts"] = "ВОЗМОЖНЫЙ ПРОХОД СКВОЗЬ ТВЁРДЫЕ ОБЪЕКТЫ"
			,["server anti-cheat flag"] = "СЕРВЕРНАЯ МЕТКА НАБЛЮДЕНИЯ"
			,["fast movement (may be game speed)"] = "БЫСТРОЕ ДВИЖЕНИЕ (ВОЗМОЖНО, СКОРОСТЬ ИГРЫ)"
			,["waiting for character"] = "ОЖИДАНИЕ ПЕРСОНАЖА"
			,["none observed. This detects replicated behavior/character anomalies, not hidden script code. Exact executor names still require server-reported ExecutorName."] = "СИГНАЛОВ НЕТ. ПРОВЕРЯЕТСЯ ТОЛЬКО СЕТЕВОЕ ПОВЕДЕНИЕ И АНОМАЛИИ ПЕРСОНАЖА, А НЕ СКРЫТЫЙ КОД."
		}
	}

	local translationBusy = {}
	local translatedObjects = {}

	local function getMenuLanguageCode()
		local code =
			string.lower(
				tostring(
					gui:GetAttribute("WelcomeLanguageCode") or "en"
				)
			)

		code = string.match(code,"^[a-z]+") or "en"

		if code ~= "ar" and code ~= "ru" then
			code = "en"
		end

		return code
	end

	local function exactTranslate(value,code)
		value = tostring(value or "")

		if code == "en" or value == "" then
			return value
		end

		local pack = exactTranslations[code]
		if not pack then
			return value
		end

		return pack[value] or value
	end

	local function translateDynamic(value,code)
		value = tostring(value or "")

		if code == "en" or value == "" then
			return value
		end

		local direct = exactTranslate(value,code)
		if direct ~= value then
			return direct
		end

		-- Session summary with a dynamic experience name and player count.
		local placeName,placePlayers =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^Place:%s*(.-)%s+|%s+Players:%s*(%d+)$"
			)

		if placeName and placePlayers then
			return
				exactTranslate("Place",code)
				..": "..placeName
				.."   |   "
				..exactTranslate("Players",code)
				..": "..placePlayers
		end

		-- Dashboard character line:
		-- HP: 100 | State: WALKING | Position: 1, 2, 3
		local hpValue,stateValue,positionValue =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^HP:%s*([^|]+)%s*|%s*State:%s*([^|]+)%s*|%s*Position:%s*(.+)$"
			)

		if hpValue and stateValue and positionValue then
			hpValue =
				string.gsub(hpValue,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")
			stateValue =
				string.gsub(stateValue,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")
			positionValue =
				string.gsub(positionValue,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")

			return
				exactTranslate("HP",code)
				..": "..hpValue
				.."   |   "
				..exactTranslate("State",code)
				..": "..exactTranslate(stateValue,code)
				.."   |   "
				..exactTranslate("Position",code)
				..": "..positionValue
		end

		-- Live HUD with changing numbers and heading.
		local hudFps,hudSpeed,hudX,hudY,hudZ,hudHeading,hudGravity =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^FPS:%s*([%-%d]+)%s+SPEED:%s*([%-%d]+)\nPOSITION:%s*X%s*([%-%d]+)%s+Y%s*([%-%d]+)%s+Z%s*([%-%d]+)\nHEADING:%s*([A-Z%-]+)%s+GRAVITY:%s*([%-%d]+)$"
			)

		if hudFps
			and hudSpeed
			and hudX
			and hudY
			and hudZ
			and hudHeading
			and hudGravity then

			return
				"FPS: "..hudFps
				.."    "
				..exactTranslate("SPEED",code)
				..": "..hudSpeed
				.."\n"
				..exactTranslate("POSITION",code)
				..": X "..hudX
				.."   Y "..hudY
				.."   Z "..hudZ
				.."\n"
				..exactTranslate("HEADING",code)
				..": "..hudHeading
				.."   "
				..exactTranslate("GRAVITY",code)
				..": "..hudGravity
		end

		-- Navigator readout with changing position and direction.
		local navX,navY,navZ,navHeading,navDegrees,navSpeed,navOrigin =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^X%s*([%-%d]+)%s+Y%s*([%-%d]+)%s+Z%s*([%-%d]+)\nHEADING%s*([A-Z]+)%s*/%s*([%d]+)°%s+SPEED%s*([%-%d]+)\nORIGIN DISTANCE%s*(.+)$"
			)

		if navX and navY and navZ and navHeading
			and navDegrees and navSpeed and navOrigin then
			return
				exactTranslate("POSITION",code)
				..": X "..navX
				.."   Y "..navY
				.."   Z "..navZ
				.."\n"
				..exactTranslate("HEADING",code)
				..": "..navHeading
				.." / "..navDegrees.."°   "
				..exactTranslate("SPEED",code)
				..": "..navSpeed
				.."\n"
				..exactTranslate("ORIGIN DISTANCE",code)
				..": "..exactTranslate(navOrigin,code)
		end

		-- Profile details with dynamic account values.
		local profileUserId,profileAge =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^USER ID:%s*(%d+)\nACCOUNT AGE:%s*(%d+)%s+DAYS$"
			)

		if profileUserId and profileAge then
			return
				exactTranslate("USER ID",code)
				..": "..profileUserId
				.."\n"
				..exactTranslate("ACCOUNT AGE",code)
				..": "..profileAge
				.." "..exactTranslate("DAYS",code)
		end

		-- Tracker status with a changing marked-player count/name.
		local markedCount =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^Tracking all players%s+|%s+Marked:%s*(.+)$"
			)

		if markedCount then
			return
				exactTranslate("Tracking all players",code)
				.."  |  "
				..exactTranslate("Marked",code)
				..": "..markedCount
		end

		local trackedName =
			string.match(value,"^Tracking only @(.*)$")

		if trackedName then
			return
				exactTranslate("Tracking only",code)
				.." @"..trackedName
		end

		-- Detector signal lists are built dynamically from English phrases.
		local signalRest =
			string.match(value,"^SIGNALS:%s*(.+)$")

		if signalRest then
			local translatedSignalRest =
				exactTranslate(signalRest,code)

			if translatedSignalRest == signalRest
				and string.find(signalRest,", ",1,true) then

				local translatedSignals = {}
				local signalStart = 1

				while true do
					local first,last =
						string.find(
							signalRest,
							", ",
							signalStart,
							true
						)

					if not first then
						table.insert(
							translatedSignals,
							exactTranslate(
								string.sub(signalRest,signalStart),
								code
							)
						)
						break
					end

					table.insert(
						translatedSignals,
						exactTranslate(
							string.sub(signalRest,signalStart,first-1),
							code
						)
					)

					signalStart = last + 1
				end

				translatedSignalRest =
					table.concat(translatedSignals,", ")
			end

			return
				exactTranslate("SIGNALS",code)
				..": "..translatedSignalRest
		end

		-- Numbered controls/statuses such as WALK 16 or SAVE PRESET 1.
		local numberedPrefix,numberedValue =
			string.match(value,"^(.-)%s+([%-]?%d+%.?%d*)$")

		if numberedPrefix and numberedValue then
			local translatedPrefix =
				exactTranslate(numberedPrefix,code)

			if translatedPrefix ~= numberedPrefix then
				return translatedPrefix.." "..numberedValue
			end
		end

		local presetSlot,presetState =
			string.match(value,"^PRESET%s+(%d+)%s+(.+)$")

		if presetSlot and presetState then
			return
				exactTranslate("PRESET",code)
				.." "..presetSlot
				.." "..exactTranslate(presetState,code)
		end

		-- FEATURE [ON/OFF]
		local feature,stateText =
			string.match(value,"^(.-) %[(.-)%]$")

		if feature and stateText then
			return
				exactTranslate(feature,code)
				.." ["
				..exactTranslate(stateText,code)
				.."]"
		end

		-- Compact feature statuses such as COMPASS HUD ON or SPRINT OFF.
		local statusFeature,statusState =
			string.match(value,"^(.-)%s+(ON|OFF)$")

		if statusFeature and statusState then
			return
				exactTranslate(statusFeature,code)
				.." "..exactTranslate(statusState,code)
		end

		-- Percentage readouts such as UI SCALE 110%.
		local percentPrefix,percentValue =
			string.match(value,"^(.-)%s+(%d+)%%$")

		if percentPrefix and percentValue then
			local translatedPrefix =
				exactTranslate(percentPrefix,code)
			if translatedPrefix ~= percentPrefix then
				return translatedPrefix.." "..percentValue.."%"
			end
		end

		-- Saved shortcut confirmations such as Flight KEYBIND = F.
		local keybindFeature,keybindValue =
			string.match(value,"^(.-)%s+KEYBIND%s*=%s*(.+)$")

		if keybindFeature and keybindValue then
			return
				exactTranslate(keybindFeature,code)
				.." "..exactTranslate("KEYBIND",code)
				.." = "..keybindValue
		end

		-- PREFIX: VALUE
		local prefix,rest =
			string.match(value,"^([^:]+):%s*(.*)$")

		if prefix and rest then
			local translatedPrefix = exactTranslate(prefix,code)
			local translatedRest = exactTranslate(rest,code)

			if translatedPrefix ~= prefix
				or translatedRest ~= rest then
				return translatedPrefix..": "..translatedRest
			end
		end

		-- STATUS // STATUS // dynamic-value
		if string.find(value," // ",1,true) then
			local pieces = {}
			local startAt = 1

			while true do
				local first,last =
					string.find(value," // ",startAt,true)

				if not first then
					table.insert(
						pieces,
						translateDynamic(
							string.sub(value,startAt),
							code
						)
					)
					break
				end

				table.insert(
					pieces,
					translateDynamic(
						string.sub(value,startAt,first-1),
						code
					)
				)

				startAt = last + 1
			end

			return table.concat(pieces," // ")
		end

		-- Safe dynamic prefixes. Only the fixed prefix is translated.
		local dynamicPrefixes = {
			"CURRENT: ",
			"PLAYERS: ",
			"DISTANCE: ",
			"USER ID: ",
			"EXECUTOR: ",
			"SIGNALS: ",
			"STATUS: ",
			"TARGET: ",
			"THEME: ",
			"OUTLINE: ",
			"TRACKING @",
			"INSPECTING @",
			"WAYPOINT SAVED // ",
			"MONSTERS FOUND: ",
			"READY // "
		}

		for _,englishPrefix in ipairs(dynamicPrefixes) do
			if string.sub(value,1,#englishPrefix) == englishPrefix then
				local basePrefix =
					string.gsub(
						englishPrefix,
						"[:/@ ]+$",
						""
					)

				local translatedPrefix =
					exactTranslate(basePrefix,code)

				if translatedPrefix ~= basePrefix then
					local suffix =
						string.sub(value,#englishPrefix+1)

					local separator =
						string.sub(
							englishPrefix,
							#basePrefix+1
						)

					return
						translatedPrefix
						..separator
						..suffix
				end
			end
		end

		-- If a phrase was not explicitly translated, keep the original
		-- English intact instead of producing a broken mixed sentence.
		return value
	end

	local function applyTranslatedObject(object,force)
		if not object
			or not object.Parent
			or translationBusy[object] then
			return
		end

		local code = getMenuLanguageCode()

		-- Keep Arabic punctuation/order readable while preserving each object's
		-- intentional left, center or right alignment.
		pcall(function()
			object.TextDirection =
				code == "ar"
				and Enum.TextDirection.RightToLeft
				or Enum.TextDirection.LeftToRight
		end)

		if object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton") then

			local last =
				object:GetAttribute("NotRubyTranslatedText")
			local source =
				object:GetAttribute("NotRubyEnglishText")

			if not force and object.Text ~= last then
				source = object.Text
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishText",
					source
				)
			elseif source == nil then
				source = object.Text
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishText",
					source
				)
			end

			local translated =
				translateDynamic(source,code)

			translationBusy[object] = true
			object:SetAttribute(
				"NotRubyTranslatedText",
				translated
			)
			object.Text = translated
			translationBusy[object] = nil

		elseif object:IsA("TextBox") then
			local last =
				object:GetAttribute(
					"NotRubyTranslatedPlaceholder"
				)
			local source =
				object:GetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishPlaceholder"
				)

			if not force
				and object.PlaceholderText ~= last then
				source = object.PlaceholderText
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishPlaceholder",
					source
				)
			elseif source == nil then
				source = object.PlaceholderText
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishPlaceholder",
					source
				)
			end

			local translated =
				translateDynamic(source,code)

			translationBusy[object] = true
			object:SetAttribute(
				"NotRubyTranslatedPlaceholder",
				translated
			)
			object.PlaceholderText = translated
			translationBusy[object] = nil
		end
	end

	local function watchTranslatedObject(object)
		if translatedObjects[object] then
			return
		end

		if not (
			object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton")
			or object:IsA("TextBox")
		) then
			return
		end

		translatedObjects[object] = true
		applyTranslatedObject(object,false)

		if object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton") then
			object:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(
				function()
					applyTranslatedObject(object,false)
				end
			)
		else
			object:GetPropertyChangedSignal(
				"PlaceholderText"
			):Connect(function()
				applyTranslatedObject(object,false)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function applyAllMenuTranslations()
		for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
			watchTranslatedObject(object)
			applyTranslatedObject(object,true)
		end
	end

	gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
		task.defer(function()
			if object and object.Parent then
				watchTranslatedObject(object)
				applyTranslatedObject(object,true)
			end
		end)
	end)

	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal(
		"WelcomeLanguageCode"
	):Connect(function()
		task.defer(applyAllMenuTranslations)
	end)

	applyAllMenuTranslations()

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Language + support
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		settingsPage,
		"LANGUAGE & SUPPORT",
		"Change the live menu language instantly. Your choice is also saved for the next launch."
	)

	local languageChoices = {
		{
			code="en",
			name="English",
			country="United States",
			aliases="english usa us america united states"
		},

	local function applyLanguageChoice()
		local query = string.lower(
			string.gsub(languageInput.Text or "","^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")
		)

		if query == "" then
			setStatus("TYPE A LANGUAGE OR COUNTRY")
			return
		end

		local choice = nil

		for _,entry in ipairs(languageChoices) do
			local haystack =
				string.lower(
					entry.code
					.." "
					..entry.name
					.." "
					..entry.country
					.." "
					..tostring(entry.aliases or "")
				)

			if query == string.lower(entry.code)
				or query == string.lower(entry.name)
				or query == string.lower(entry.country)
				or string.find(haystack,query,1,true) then
				choice = entry
				break
			end
		end

		if not choice then
			setStatus("LANGUAGE NOT FOUND")
			return
		end

		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeLanguageCode",choice.code)
		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeLanguageName",choice.name)
		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeCountry",choice.country)

		if type(writefile) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				writefile(languageFile,choice.code)
			end)
		end

		languageInput.Text = ""
		languageCurrent.Text = "CURRENT: "..choice.name
		applyAllMenuTranslations()
		setStatus("LANGUAGE CHANGED // "..string.upper(choice.name))
	end

	languageApply.Activated:Connect(applyLanguageChoice)

	languageInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
		if enterPressed then
			applyLanguageChoice()
		end
	end)

	-- Custom color picker // touch-friendly circular palette
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		settingsPage,
